Control.submit 是否真正单事务：动作回执去重（action_id；重复动作返回原回执）、事件、任务/共享空间变更、QUEUED 执行意图是否同一事务提交；有无先持久化意图后写业务状态的窗口。\n2. 崩溃窗口（§9.2/T8/T21）：动作提交后回执丢失、事件已写但投递未确认、回合中途死亡等路径；恢复核对逻辑是否存在「消费位置先推进、后确认」类错误顺序；重复投递是否可能重复注入上下文。\n3. 投递批次号与 context_epoch（§7/T24）：新会话隔离、同名新成员不继承旧身份的实现是否完备；事件受众/版本快照语义（新增成员/通道不补发历史）。\n4. 拓扑 patch（§8/T11-T13）：base_revision 冲突、受影响集合计算、DRAINING 与边界等待、原子应用（不半应用）、权限上限校验、并发 patch 竞态；新增成员是否能在 Leader 同一回合内立即被委派（无死锁）。\n5. 资源上限（§6.4/T22）：max_turns_per_goal 计数在崩溃恢复后是否重复计数、LIMIT_REACHED 是否可能被冒充为完成、并发计数是否准确。\n6. 状态机合法性（§5.3）：任务/回合/目标/团队状态转换是否存在非法路径（完成与取消竞态、结果不明处理）；失败状态是否可能被静默吞掉。\n7. models.py：TeamSpec/动作/事件的校验完备性（未知字段、非法引用、ACL 一致性、观察者能力）；序列化/反序列化一致性。\n8. SQLite 实现细节：WAL/busy_timeout/事务边界/索引/并发写；JSON 字段解析容错；长事务风险。\n\n方法：通读实现后，对可疑点用 grep 追调用点、写最小复现脚本到 review/tmp/（不得改仓库源码）或运行相关确定性测试。每条发现给 file:line、影响、建议修复、严重度（P0-P3）。宁缺毋滥，禁止臆测；未能验证的标注「待验证」。\n\n验收标准：review/findings-core.md 按协议结构完整；每条发现可核验；明确列出未验证项；向 leader 发消息（一句话结论+Top5 发现+路径）；不修改任何被审文件（可用 git status 自证）。", "acceptance": "", "status": "RUNNING", "requester": "leader"}]&lt;/your_tasks&gt;

<message type="tool"> 1  # TeamAgents 代码审查协议（v1）
 2  
 3  &gt; 本文件由 Leader 制定，所有审查员必须遵守。基准：`TeamAgents-Implementation-Plan.zh-CN.md`。
 4  
 5  ## 目标
 6  
 7  对 TeamAgents 仓库当前代码实现做系统审查：正确性、安全性、持久化/恢复、并发、与基准方案的一致性、测试证据可信度。产出：每位审查员一份 `review/findings-&lt;area&gt;.md`；Leader 汇总为 `review/REVIEW-REPORT.md`。
 8  
 9  ## 硬性规则
10  
11  1. **只读**：不得修改 `src/**`、`tests/**`、`docs/**`、`examples/**`、`pyproject.toml`、`uv.lock` 等仓库文件；只能在 `review/**` 下新建/写入文件（临时脚本放 `review/tmp/`，可清理）。
12  2. **不跑 live 测试**（沙箱无密钥、无网络）：不要用 `-m live`；不要联网。确定性套件可运行：
13     `.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q`
14     已知环境性失败（沙箱造成，不算代码缺陷，除非你能证明另有原因）：
15     - `tests/test_config_cli.py::test_xhigh_maps_to_max_for_models_without_xhigh`（缺 `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`）
16     - `tests/test_p3_web_tools.py::test_ssrf_guard_blocks_internal_targets`（无 DNS，无法解析 example.com）
17  3. **每条发现必须可核验**：给 `file:line`、代码摘录或命令、影响、建议修复、严重度。禁止无证据的泛泛结论；推测标注「待验证」。
18  4. 区分三类问题：
19     - A 与基准方案/文档声称不符；
20     - B 实现缺陷（正确性/安全/并发/恢复/错误处理）；
21     - C 通用质量问题（可维护性、死代码、边界、测试薄弱）。
22  5. 报告用中文，固定结构：
23     - 一、范围与方法（读了哪些文件、跑了什么命令）
24     - 二、结论（3–5 行）
25     - 三、发现清单（ID｜严重度｜标题｜证据 `file:line`｜影响｜建议）
26     - 四、与方案/文档的偏差
27     - 五、未验证/存疑项
28     - 六、自检（实际运行过的命令与结果）
29  6. 完成后向 Leader 发消息（`send_message`）：一句话结论 + Top 5 发现（ID/严重度/标题）+ 报告路径。**不要**发送完整报告正文。
30  7. 交叉检查：其他审查员的报告会陆续出现在 `review/findings-*.md`，你可以阅读以对齐口径，但不得改写他人文件。
31  
32  ## 审查基准（必须对照）
33  
34  - `TeamAgents-Implementation-Plan.zh-CN.md`：§2.2 八条核心约束、DP-1..12、§4 执行模型、§5 状态机、§6.4 资源上限、§7 团队动作与信息边界、§8 拓扑变更、§9 持久化/恢复/取消、§10 适配器、§11 模型、§12 工具权限与工作目录、§13 入口、§14 配置、§16–17 阶段与验收（T1–T24）。
35  - `docs/DECISIONS.md`：已确认偏离（D-1..D-9）；代码若偏离方案但无 DECISIONS 记录 = 发现。
36  - `docs/STATUS.md`、`docs/ACCEPTANCE.md`：对状态/证据的声称是否被代码与测试支撑。
37  
38  ## 严重度参考
39  
40  - **P0**：安全边界可绕过（权限/隔离/ACL/SSRF/密钥）、数据损坏或恢复错误、把失败/取消/超限谎报为完成、审计可信度问题。
41  - **P1**：关键语义错误、竞态、恢复窗口漏洞、异常被吞导致状态不可恢复。
42  - **P2**：边界/错误处理不当、文档与实现明显不符、测试薄弱可能掩盖回归。
43  - **P3**：可维护性/一致性/清理类。
44  
45  ## 汇总约定
46  
47  - 报告路径：
48    - core 域 → `review/findings-core.md`
49    - runtime 域 → `review/findings-runtime.md`
50    - adapters 域 → `review/findings-adapters.md`
51    - surface 域 → `review/findings-surface.md`
52    - verify 域 → `review/findings-verify.md`
53  - 另将「一句话结论 + 发现计数（P0/P1/P2/P3）」发布到 `review` 共享空间（`publish_shared`），便于交叉阅读。
54  - 只报告**你亲自读过并理解**的代码；没读到的模块不要臆断。</message>

<message type="tool">  1  """SQLite business database: the authority for team facts and action receipts.
  2  
  3  One writer per session (the session runtime serializes via an asyncio lock),
  4  transactions + constraints, WAL, busy timeout (plan section 9.1).
  5  """
  6  
  7  from __future__ import annotations
  8  
  9  import json
 10  import sqlite3
 11  import threading
 12  import time
 13  from pathlib import Path
 14  from typing import Any, Iterable
 15  
 16  from .models import (
 17      AgentStatus,
 18      ApprovalRequest,
 19      ApprovalStatus,
 20      PatchStatus,
 21      Receipt,
 22      SessionStatus,
 23      SharedEntry,
 24      Task,
 25      TaskStatus,
 26      TeamEvent,
 27      TeamSpec,
 28      TopologyPatch,
 29      TurnRun,
 30      TurnStatus,
 31  )
 32  
 33  DB_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1
 34  
 35  SCHEMA = """
 36  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL);
 37  
 38  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions(
 39    session_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 40    status TEXT NOT NULL,
 41    cwd TEXT NOT NULL,
 42    permissions_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'approved_scope',
 43    goal_id TEXT,
 44    goal_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'idle',
 45    created_at REAL NOT NULL,
 46    updated_at REAL NOT NULL
 47  );
 48  
 49  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS team_specs(
 50    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
 51    revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
 52    spec_json TEXT NOT NULL,
 53    created_at REAL NOT NULL,
 54    PRIMARY KEY(session_id, revision)
 55  );
 56  
 57  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks(
 58    task_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 59    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
 60    parent_task_id TEXT,
 61    goal_id TEXT,
 62    requester TEXT NOT NULL,
 63    assignee TEXT NOT NULL,
 64    description TEXT NOT NULL,
 65    acceptance TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
 66    dependencies TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
 67    status TEXT NOT NULL,
 68    result_refs TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
 69    cancel_requested INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
 70    created_at REAL NOT NULL,
 71    updated_at REAL NOT NULL
 72  );
 73  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tasks_session ON tasks(session_id, status);
 74  
 75  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS turn_runs(
 76    run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 77    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
 78    task_id TEXT,
 79    goal_id TEXT,
 80    agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,
 81    config_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
 82    topology_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
 83    status TEXT NOT NULL,
 84    input_delivery_ids TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
 85    context_ref TEXT,
 86    external_turn_id TEXT,
 87    cancel_requested INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
 88    waiting_on TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
 89    created_at REAL NOT NULL,
 90    updated_at REAL NOT NULL
 91  );
 92  -- at most one active turn per member (plan section 2.2 rule 5)
 93  CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uniq_active_run
 94    ON turn_runs(agent_id) WHERE status = 'RUNNING';
 95  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_runs_session ON turn_runs(session_id, status);
 96  
 97  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS actions(
 98    action_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 99    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
100    actor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
101    run_id TEXT,
102    kind TEXT NOT NULL,
103    payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
104    receipt_json TEXT NOT NULL,
105    created_at REAL NOT NULL
106  );
107  
108  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events(
109    sequence INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
110    event_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
111    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
112    actor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
113    task_id TEXT,
114    kind TEXT NOT NULL,
115    payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
116    audience_json TEXT NOT NULL,
117    topology_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
118    causation_id TEXT,
119    created_at REAL NOT NULL
120  );
121  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_session ON events(session_id, sequence);
122  
123  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS deliveries(
124    delivery_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
125    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
126    agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,
127    event_id TEXT NOT NULL,
128    batch_no INTEGER NOT NULL,
129    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
130    payload_override TEXT,
131    created_at REAL NOT NULL,
132    applied_at REAL
133  );
134  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_deliveries_agent ON deliveries(session_id, agent_id, status);
135  
136  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_runtime(
137    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
138    agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,
139    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'IDLE',
140    config_revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
141    context_epoch INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

243  class TeamSpec(BaseModel):
244      """Team structure as validated data (DP-1). Never generated code."""
245  
246      model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
247  
248      schema_version: int = SCHEMA_VERSION
249      leader_id: str
250      agents: list[AgentSpec]
251      channels: list[ChannelSpec] = Field(default_factory=list)
252      observers: list[ObserverSpec] = Field(default_factory=list)
253      shared_spaces: list[SharedSpaceSpec] = Field(default_factory=list)
254      limits: Limits = Field(default_factory=Limits)
255  
256      # -- reference validation (plan section 5.2) ----------------------------
257  
258      @model_validator(mode="after")
259      def _validate_refs(self) -&gt; "TeamSpec":
260          ids = [a.id for a in self.agents]
261          if len(ids) != len(set(ids)):
262              raise ValueError("duplicate agent ids in team spec")
263          if self.leader_id not in ids:
264              raise ValueError(f"leader_id {self.leader_id!r} is not a member")
265          leaders = [a for a in self.agents if a.id == self.leader_id]
266          if len(leaders) != 1 or leaders[0].role != "leader":
267              raise ValueError("exactly one member with role 'leader' must exist")
268          if len(self.agents) &gt; self.limits.max_members:
269              raise ValueError(
270                  f"team has {len(self.agents)} members, limit is {self.limits.max_members}"
271              )
272          known = set(ids)
273          for ch in self.channels:
274              if ch.source not in known:
275                  raise ValueError(f"channel source {ch.source!r} is not a member")
276              for t in ch.targets:
277                  if t not in known:
278                      raise ValueError(f"channel target {t!r} is not a member")
279          for ob in self.observers:
280              if ob.agent_id not in known:
281                  raise ValueError(f"observer {ob.agent_id!r} is not a member")
282              for s in ob.subjects:
283                  if s not in known:
284                      raise ValueError(f"observer subject {s!r} is not a member")
285          spaces = [s.id for s in self.shared_spaces]
286          if len(spaces) != len(set(spaces)):
287              raise ValueError("duplicate shared space ids")
288          for sp in self.shared_spaces:
289              for who in sp.readers + sp.writers:
290                  if who not in known:
291                      raise ValueError(f"shared space {sp.id!r} references unknown member {who!r}")
292          return self
293  
294      # -- lookups -------------------------------------------------------------
295  
296      def agent(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; AgentSpec:
297          for a in self.agents:
298              if a.id == agent_id:
299                  return a
300          raise ValueError(f"unknown member {agent_id!r}")
301  
302      @property
303      def leader(self) -&gt; AgentSpec:
304          return self.agent(self.leader_id)
305  
306      @property
307      def agent_ids(self) -&gt; list[str]:
308          return [a.id for a in self.agents]
309  
310      def can_send(self, source: str, target: str) -&gt; bool:
311          """Directed send rights: channel entry with mode message/broadcast."""
312          for ch in self.channels:
313              if ch.source != source:
314                  continue
315              if ch.mode is ChannelMode.TASK:
316                  continue
317              if ch.mode is ChannelMode.BROADCAST or target in ch.targets:
318                  return True
319          return False
320  
321      def can_delegate(self, source: str, target: str) -&gt; bool:
322          """Task delegation rights: channel entry with mode task, or leader."""
323          if source == self.leader_id:
324              return True
325          for ch in self.channels:
326              if ch.source == source and ch.mode is ChannelMode.TASK and target in ch.targets:
327                  return True
328          return False
329  
330      def space(self, space_id: str) -&gt; SharedSpaceSpec:
331          for s in self.shared_spaces:
332              if s.id == space_id:
333                  return s
334          raise ValueError(f"unknown shared space {space_id!r}")

217  class Store:
218      """Thin synchronous SQLite wrapper. The caller serializes writes."""
219  
220      def __init__(self, path: str | Path):
221          self.path = str(path)
222          if self.path != ":memory:":
223              Path(self.path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
224          # tool threads may reach the store (langchain runs sync tools in a pool),
225          # so the connection is shared but every statement is serialized by a lock
226          self._lock = threading.RLock()
227          self.conn = _LockedConn(
228              sqlite3.connect(self.path, isolation_level=None, check_same_thread=False),
229              self._lock)
230          self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
231          self.conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
232          self.conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
233          self.conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000")
234          self.conn.executescript(SCHEMA)
235          self._tx_depth = 0
236          self._check_schema_version()
237  
238      def _check_schema_version(self) -&gt; None:
239          row = self.conn.execute("SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key='db_schema_version'").fetchone()
240          if row is None:
241              self.conn.execute(
242                  "INSERT INTO meta(key, value) VALUES('db_schema_version', ?)",
243                  (str(DB_SCHEMA_VERSION),),
244              )
245          elif int(row["value"]) != DB_SCHEMA_VERSION:
246              raise RuntimeError(
247                  f"database schema version {row['value']} != supported {DB_SCHEMA_VERSION}; "
248                  "back up and migrate before opening"
249              )
250  
251      def close(self) -&gt; None:
252          self.conn.close()
253  
254      # -- transactions --------------------------------------------------------
255  
256      def tx(self):
257          return _Tx(self)
258  
259      # -- sessions -------------------------------------------------------------
260  
261      def create_session(self, session_id: str, cwd: str, permissions_mode: str) -&gt; None:
262          t = time.time()
263          with self.tx():
264              self.conn.execute(
265                  "INSERT INTO sessions(session_id, status, cwd, permissions_mode, created_at, updated_at)"
266                  " VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)",
267                  (session_id, SessionStatus.ACTIVE, cwd, permissions_mode, t, t),
268              )
269  
270      def get_session(self, session_id: str) -&gt; sqlite3.Row | None:
271          return self.conn.execute(
272              "SELECT * FROM sessions WHERE session_id=?", (session_id,)
273          ).fetchone()
274  
275      def set_session_status(self, session_id: str, status: str) -&gt; None:
276          with self.tx():
277              self.conn.execute(
278                  "UPDATE sessions SET status=?, updated_at=? WHERE session_id=?",
279                  (status, time.time(), session_id),
280              )
281  
282      def set_goal_state(self, session_id: str, goal_id: str, state: str) -&gt; None:
283          with self.tx():
284              self.conn.execute(
285                  "UPDATE sessions SET goal_id=?, goal_state=?, updated_at=? WHERE session_id=?",
286                  (goal_id, state, time.time(), session_id),
287              )
288  
289      def set_permission_mode(self, session_id: str, mode: str) -&gt; None:
290          with self.tx():
291              self.conn.execute(
292                  "UPDATE sessions SET permissions_mode=?, updated_at=? WHERE session_id=?",
293                  (mode, time.time(), session_id),
294              )
295  
296      # -- meta ----------------------------------------------------------------
297  
298      def get_meta(self, key: str) -&gt; str | None:
299          row = self.conn.execute("SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key=?", (key,)).fetchone()
300          return row["value"] if row else None
301  
302      def set_meta(self, key: str, value: str) -&gt; None:
303          self.conn.execute(
304              "INSERT INTO meta(key, value) VALUES(?,?)"
305              " ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value",
306              (key, value),
307          )
308  
309      def del_meta(self, key: str) -&gt; None:
310          self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM meta WHERE key=?", (key,))
311  
312      # -- team spec -----------------------------------------------------------
313  
314      def save_team_spec(self, session_id: str, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; int:
315          rev = self.current_revision(session_id) + 1
316          with self.tx():
317              self.conn.execute(
318                  "INSERT INTO team_specs(session_id, revision, spec_json, created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?)",
319                  (session_id, rev, spec.model_dump_json(), time.time()),
320              )
321          return rev
322  
323      def current_revision(self, session_id: str) -&gt; int:
324          row = self.conn.execute(
325              "SELECT MAX(revision) AS r FROM team_specs WHERE session_id=?", (session_id,)
326          ).fetchone()
327          return int(row["r"] or 0)
328

347  
348      def record_action(self, action_id: str, session_id: str, actor_id: str,
349                        run_id: str | None, kind: str, payload_hash: str, receipt: Receipt) -&gt; None:
350          self.conn.execute(

356  
357      # -- events and deliveries ----------------------------------------------
358  
359      def append_event(self, event: TeamEvent) -&gt; int:
360          """Insert event, return its sequence. Caller holds the transaction."""
361          cur = self.conn.execute(
362              "INSERT INTO events(event_id, session_id, actor_id, task_id, kind, payload_json,"
363              " audience_json, topology_revision, causation_id, created_at)"
364              " VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
365              (event.event_id, event.session_id, event.actor_id, event.task_id, event.kind,
366               json.dumps(event.payload), json.dumps(event.audience),
367               event.topology_revision, event.causation_id, event.created_at),
368          )
369          return int(cur.lastrowid)
370  
371      def events(self, session_id: str, after_sequence: int = 0, limit: int = 1000) -&gt; list[sqlite3.Row]:
372          return self.conn.execute(
373              "SELECT * FROM events WHERE session_id=? AND sequence&gt;? ORDER BY sequence LIMIT ?",
374              (session_id, after_sequence, limit),
375          ).fetchall()
376  
377      def create_delivery(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str, event_id: str, batch_no: int,
378                          payload_override: dict | None = None) -&gt; int:
379          cur = self.conn.execute(
380              "INSERT INTO deliveries(session_id, agent_id, event_id, batch_no, status,"
381              " payload_override, created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?, 'pending', ?, ?)",
382              (session_id, agent_id, event_id, batch_no,
383               json.dumps(payload_override) if payload_override is not None else None,
384               time.time()),
385          )
386          return int(cur.lastrowid)
387  
388      def next_batch_no(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str) -&gt; int:
389          """Caller holds the transaction. Monotonic per member."""
390          row = self.conn.execute(
391              "SELECT next_batch_no FROM agent_runtime WHERE session_id=? AND agent_id=?",
392              (session_id, agent_id),
393          ).fetchone()
394          batch = int(row["next_batch_no"]) if row else 1
395          self.conn.execute(
396              "INSERT INTO agent_runtime(session_id, agent_id, next_batch_no, updated_at)"
397              " VALUES(?,?,?,?)"
398              " ON CONFLICT(session_id, agent_id) DO UPDATE SET next_batch_no=excluded.next_batch_no,"
399              " updated_at=excluded.updated_at",

401          )
402          return batch

404      def pending_deliveries(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str) -&gt; list[sqlite3.Row]:
405          return self.conn.execute(
406              "SELECT d.*, e.kind AS event_kind, e.payload_json, e.actor_id AS event_actor,"
407              " e.task_id AS event_task_id, e.sequence AS event_sequence, e.audience_json"
408              " FROM deliveries d JOIN events e ON e.event_id = d.event_id"
409              " WHERE d.session_id=? AND d.agent_id=? AND d.status='pending'"
410              " ORDER BY d.batch_no, e.sequence",
411              (session_id, agent_id),
412          ).fetchall()

418          rows = self.conn.execute(
419              f"SELECT e.kind FROM deliveries d JOIN events e ON e.event_id=d.event_id"
420              f" WHERE d.delivery_id IN ({marks}) ORDER BY e.sequence",
421              tuple(delivery_ids),
422          ).fetchall()

432      def ack_deliveries(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str, batch_no: int) -&gt; int:
433          """Record that a member applied a delivery batch; advance consume cursor."""
434          with self.tx():
435              cur = self.conn.execute(
436                  "UPDATE deliveries SET status='applied', applied_at=?"
437                  " WHERE session_id=? AND agent_id=? AND batch_no&lt;=? AND status='pending'",
438                  (time.time(), session_id, agent_id, batch_no),
439              )
440              self.conn.execute(
441                  "INSERT INTO agent_runtime(session_id, agent_id, last_applied_batch, updated_at)"
442                  " VALUES(?,?,?,?)"
443                  " ON CONFLICT(session_id, agent_id) DO UPDATE SET"
444                  " last_applied_batch=MAX(last_applied_batch, excluded.last_applied_batch),"
445                  " updated_at=excluded.updated_at",
446                  (session_id, agent_id, batch_no, time.time()),
447              )
448              return cur.rowcount

450      def ack_run_deliveries(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; None:
451          """Acknowledge every delivery the member applied during this run."""
452          if not run.input_delivery_ids:
453              return
454          marks = ",".join("?" * len(run.input_delivery_ids))
455          row = self.conn.execute(
456              f"SELECT MAX(batch_no) AS b FROM deliveries WHERE delivery_id IN ({marks})",
457              tuple(run.input_delivery_ids),
458          ).fetchone()
459          if row and row["b"] is not None:
460              self.ack_deliveries(run.session_id, run.agent_id, int(row["b"]))

462      def waiters_for_task(self, session_id: str, task_id: str) -&gt; list[str]:
463          """Members parked in WAITING_TASK on this task (to wake with the result)."""
464          out = []

466              if task_id in run.waiting_on:
467                  out.append(run.agent_id)

549      def compare_and_set_task(self, task_id: str, expected: str, new: str,
550                               result_refs: list[str] | None = None) -&gt; bool:
551          """Compare-and-swap on task status; returns False on version mismatch."""
552          refs = json.dumps(result_refs) if result_refs is not None else None
553          if refs is None:
554              cur = self.conn.execute(
555                  "UPDATE tasks SET status=?, updated_at=? WHERE task_id=? AND status=?",
556                  (new, time.time(), task_id, expected),
557              )
558          else:
559              cur = self.conn.execute(
560                  "UPDATE tasks SET status=?, result_refs=?, updated_at=?"
561                  " WHERE task_id=? AND status=?",
562                  (new, refs, time.time(), task_id, expected),
563              )
564          return cur.rowcount == 1

572      def reassign_tasks(self, assignee: str, new_assignee: str, statuses: Iterable[str]) -&gt; list[str]:
573          marks = ",".join("?" * len(statuses))
574          rows = self.conn.execute(
575              f"SELECT task_id FROM tasks WHERE assignee=? AND status IN ({marks})",
576              (assignee, *statuses),
577          ).fetchall()
578          ids = [r["task_id"] for r in rows]
579          with self.tx():
580              for tid in ids:
581                  self.conn.execute(
582                      "UPDATE tasks SET assignee=?, updated_at=? WHERE task_id=?",
583                      (new_assignee, time.time(), tid),
584                  )
585          return ids

 944  class _Tx:
 945      """Re-entrant transaction context manager (BEGIN IMMEDIATE at depth 0).
 946  
 947      Re-entrancy lets control-layer methods compose store operations into one
 948      business transaction without nesting BEGIN statements.
 949      """
 950  
 951      def __init__(self, store: "Store"):
 952          self.store = store
 953          self.conn = store.conn
 954  
 955      def __enter__(self):
 956          self.store._lock.acquire()
 957          if self.store._tx_depth == 0:
 958              self.conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
 959          self.store._tx_depth += 1
 960          return self.conn
 961  
 962      def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
 963          self.store._tx_depth -= 1
 964          if self.store._tx_depth &gt; 0:
 965              self.store._lock.release()
 966              return False
 967          if exc_type is None:
 968              self.conn.execute("COMMIT")
 969          else:
 970              self.conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
 971          self.store._lock.release()
 972          return False
 973  
 974  
 975  class _LockedConn:
 976      """sqlite3 connection whose statements are serialized across threads."""
 977  
 978      def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, lock: threading.RLock):
 979          object.__setattr__(self, "_conn", conn)
 980          object.__setattr__(self, "_lock", lock)
 981  
 982      def execute(self, *args):
 983          with self._lock:
 984              return self._conn.execute(*args)
 985  
 986      def executemany(self, *args):
 987          with self._lock:
 988              return self._conn.executemany(*args)
 989  
 990      def executescript(self, *args):
 991          with self._lock:
 992              return self._conn.executescript(*args)
 993  
 994      def close(self) -&gt; None:
 995          with self._lock:
 996              self._conn.close()
 997  
 998      def __getattr__(self, name):
 999          return getattr(self._conn, name)
1000  
1001      def __setattr__(self, name, value) -&gt; None:
1002          setattr(self._conn, name, value)</message>

755          ).fetchone()
756          return bool(row["cancel_requested"]) if row else False
757  
758      def task_cancel_requested(self, task_id: str) -&gt; bool:
759          row = self.conn.execute(
760              "SELECT cancel_requested FROM tasks WHERE task_id=?", (task_id,)
761          ).fetchone()
762          return bool(row["cancel_requested"]) if row else False
763  
764      # -- completion requests -------------------------------------------------
765  
766      def record_completion_request(self, run_id: str, task_id: str,
767                                    result_refs: list[str], summary: str) -&gt; None:
768          self.conn.execute(
769              "INSERT INTO completion_requests(run_id, task_id, result_refs, summary, created_at)"
770              " VALUES(?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(run_id) DO UPDATE SET"
771              " task_id=excluded.task_id, result_refs=excluded.result_refs, summary=excluded.summary",
772              (run_id, task_id, json.dumps(result_refs), summary, time.time()),
773          )
774  
775      def completion_request(self, run_id: str) -&gt; sqlite3.Row | None:
776          return self.conn.execute(
777              "SELECT * FROM completion_requests WHERE run_id=?", (run_id,)
778          ).fetchone()
779  
780      # -- shared space --------------------------------------------------------
781  
782      def add_shared_entry(self, entry: SharedEntry, session_id: str) -&gt; int:
783          cur = self.conn.execute(
784              "INSERT INTO shared_entries(entry_id, session_id, space_id, author, kind, content,"
785              " ref, supersedes, created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
786              (entry.entry_id, session_id, entry.space_id, entry.author, entry.kind, entry.content,
787               entry.ref, entry.supersedes, entry.created_at),
788          )
789          return int(cur.lastrowid)
790  
791      def shared_entries(self, session_id: str, space_ids: Iterable[str],
792                         after_sequence: int = 0, limit: int = 100) -&gt; list[SharedEntry]:
793          ids = list(space_ids)
794          if not ids:
795              return []
796          marks = ",".join("?" * len(ids))
797          rows = self.conn.execute(
798              f"SELECT * FROM shared_entries WHERE session_id=? AND space_id IN ({marks})"
799              " AND sequence&gt;? ORDER BY sequence LIMIT ?",
800              (session_id, *ids, after_sequence, limit),
801          ).fetchall()
802          return [self._row_to_shared(r) for r in rows]
803  
804      def shared_cursor(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str, space_id: str) -&gt; int:
805          row = self.conn.execute(
806              "SELECT sequence FROM shared_cursors WHERE session_id=? AND agent_id=? AND space_id=?",
807              (session_id, agent_id, space_id),
808          ).fetchone()
809          return int(row["sequence"]) if row else 0
810  
811      def advance_shared_cursor(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str, space_id: str,
812                                sequence: int) -&gt; None:
813          self.conn.execute(
814              "INSERT INTO shared_cursors(session_id, agent_id, space_id, sequence) VALUES(?,?,?,?)"
815              " ON CONFLICT(session_id, agent_id, space_id) DO UPDATE SET"
816              " sequence=MAX(sequence, excluded.sequence)",
817              (session_id, agent_id, space_id, sequence),
818          )
819  
820      @staticmethod
821      def _row_to_shared(row: sqlite3.Row) -&gt; SharedEntry:
822          return SharedEntry(
823              entry_id=row["entry_id"], space_id=row["space_id"], author=row["author"],
824              kind=row["kind"], content=row["content"], ref=row["ref"],
825              supersedes=row["supersedes"], sequence=row["sequence"], created_at=row["created_at"],
826          )
827  
828      # -- approvals -----------------------------------------------------------
829  
830      def insert_approval(self, req: ApprovalRequest) -&gt; None:
831          self.conn.execute(

833              " operation_hash, requested_scope, policy_revision, status, created_at)"
834              " VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",

836               req.operation_hash, json.dumps(req.requested_scope), req.policy_revision,
837               req.status, req.created_at),
838          )
839  
840      def decide_approval(self, approval_id: str, status: ApprovalStatus) -&gt; bool:
841          cur = self.conn.execute(
842              "UPDATE approvals SET status=?, decided_at=? WHERE approval_id=? AND status='PENDING'",
843              (status, time.time(), approval_id),
844          )
845          return cur.rowcount == 1
846  
847      def get_approval(self, approval_id: str) -&gt; ApprovalRequest | None:
848          row = self.conn.execute(
849              "SELECT * FROM approvals WHERE approval_id=?", (approval_id,)
850          ).fetchone()
851          if not row:

853          return ApprovalRequest(

855              agent_id=row["agent_id"], run_id=row["run_id"], tool_call_id=row["tool_call_id"],
856              operation_hash=row["operation_hash"],
857              requested_scope=json.loads(row["requested_scope"]),
858              policy_revision=row["policy_revision"], status=ApprovalStatus(row["status"]),
859              created_at=row["created_at"], decided_at=row["decided_at"],
860          )
861  
862      def pending_approvals(self, session_id: str) -&gt; list[ApprovalRequest]:
863          rows = self.conn.execute(
864              "SELECT approval_id FROM approvals WHERE session_id=? AND status='PENDING'",
865              (session_id,),
866          ).fetchall()
867          return [self.get_approval(r["approval_id"]) for r in rows]

<message type="tool">  1  """Team control flow: ingest -&gt; validate -&gt; reduce -&gt; schedule -&gt; persist.
  2  
  3  Every team action enters here exactly once; the business transaction (receipt,
  4  events, task/shared-space changes, queued execution intents) commits together.
  5  Action ids are stable (`run_id:tool_call_id`), repeats return the original receipt.
  6  """
  7  
  8  from __future__ import annotations
  9  
 10  import hashlib
 11  import json
 12  from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 13  from typing import Any
 14  
 15  from . import views
 16  from .models import (
 17      ActionKind,
 18      AgentSpec,
 19      AgentStatus,
 20      ApprovalStatus,
 21      ChannelSpec,
 22      EventKind,
 23      Limits,
 24      PatchStatus,
 25      Receipt,
 26      SessionStatus,
 27      SharedEntry,
 28      Task,
 29      TaskStatus,
 30      TeamAction,
 31      TeamEvent,
 32      TeamSpec,
 33      TopologyPatch,
 34      TurnRun,
 35      TurnStatus,
 36      TURN_TERMINAL_STATUSES,
 37      UserConfig,
 38      new_id,
 39      now,
 40  )
 41  from .storage import Store
 42  
 43  #: capabilities the runtime provides natively; they need no user-config entry
 44  BUILTIN_TOOL_BINDINGS = {"files", "shell", "web"}
 45  
 46  
 47  @dataclass
 48  class EventDraft:
 49      kind: EventKind
 50      payload: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
 51      task_id: str | None = None
 52      targets: list[str] | None = None
 53      actor_id: str | None = None
 54      audience: list[str] | None = None
 55      push: list[str] | None = None
 56  
 57  
 58  @dataclass
 59  class Reduction:
 60      events: list[EventDraft]
 61      receipt: Receipt
 62  
 63  
 64  class Control:
 65      """The single serialized entry point for team transactions per session."""
 66  
 67      def __init__(self, store: Store, session_id: str, catalog: UserConfig | None = None):
 68          self.store = store
 69          self.session_id = session_id
 70          self.catalog = catalog or UserConfig()
 71          self.last_schedule_error: str | None = None
 72          #: (run_id, inbox items) recorded when a running member receives mid-turn input
 73          self.mid_turn_pushes: list[tuple[str, list[dict]]] = []
 74  
 75      # ------------------------------------------------------------------ submit
 76  
 77      def submit(self, action: TeamAction) -&gt; Receipt:
 78          with self.store.tx():
 79              prior = self.store.get_action_receipt(action.action_id)
 80              if prior is not None:
 81                  return prior
 82              spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)
 83              try:
 84                  error = self._validate(action, spec)
 85                  if error is not None:
 86                      receipt = Receipt.failure(action, error)
 87                      self.store.record_action(
 88                          action.action_id, self.session_id, action.actor_id, action.run_id,
 89                          action.kind, self._payload_hash(action), receipt,
 90                      )
 91                      return receipt
 92                  reduction = self._reduce(action, spec)
 93              except Exception as e:
 94                  # malformed model input is a readable refusal, never a crash:
 95                  # the transaction rolls back and nothing half-applies
 96                  receipt = Receipt.failure(action, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")
 97                  self.store.record_action(
 98                      action.action_id, self.session_id, action.actor_id, action.run_id,
 99                      action.kind, self._payload_hash(action), receipt,
100                  )
101                  return receipt
102              self._persist_events(action, spec, reduction.events)
103              self._schedule(spec)
104              self.store.record_action(
105                  action.action_id, self.session_id, action.actor_id, action.run_id,
106                  action.kind, self._payload_hash(action), reduction.receipt,
107              )
108              return reduction.receipt
109  
110      @staticmethod
111      def _derived_task_id(action: TeamAction) -&gt; str:
112          """Deterministic per action id, unique even for several tasks in one run."""
113          if explicit := action.payload.get("task_id"):
114              return str(explicit)
115          digest = hashlib.sha256(action.action_id.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
116          return f"task_{digest}"
117  
118      @staticmethod
119      def _payload_hash(action: TeamAction) -&gt; str:
120          blob = json.dumps(action.payload, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
121          return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode()).hexdigest()[:32]
122  
123      # ------------------------------------------------------------ public API
124  
125      def schedule(self) -&gt; None:
126          """Re-run scheduling against the committed state (called after turn ends)."""
127          with self.store.tx():
128              self._schedule(self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id))
129  
130      def emit(self, drafts: list[EventDraft], actor_id: str = "system") -&gt; None:
131          """Persist runtime-originated events (run/task lifecycle) and schedule."""
132          with self.store.tx():
133              spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)
134              action = TeamAction(action_id=new_id("sys"), session_id=self.session_id,
135                                  actor_id=actor_id, kind=ActionKind.SEND_MESSAGE, payload={})
136              self._persist_events(action, spec, drafts)
137              self._schedule(spec)
138  
139      # -------------------------------------------------------------- validation
140  
141      def _validate(self, action: TeamAction, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; str | None:
142          kind = action.kind
143          member_ids = {a.id for a in spec.agents}
144          actor = action.actor_id
145  
146          if kind is ActionKind.USER_MESSAGE or kind is ActionKind.USER_SUPPLEMENT:
147              if actor != "user":
148                  return "only the local user can submit user input"
149              if not str(action.payload.get("text", "")).strip():
150                  return "user input text must not be empty"

548                      receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,
549                                      result={"mode": p["mode"]}),
550                  )
551  
552              case ActionKind.PAUSE_SESSION:
553                  self.store.set_session_status(self.session_id, SessionStatus.PAUSED)
554                  return Reduction(
555                      events=[EventDraft(kind=EventKind.SESSION_STATUS,
556                                         payload={"status": "PAUSED"})],
557                      receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,
558                                      result={"status": "PAUSED"}),
559                  )
560  
561          return Reduction(
562              events=[],
563              receipt=Receipt.failure(action, f"unsupported action kind {kind}"),
564          )

566      # ------------------------------------------------------------- reductions
567  
568      def _read_shared(self, action: TeamAction, spec: TeamSpec,
569                       advance: bool) -&gt; Reduction:
570          actor = action.actor_id
571          p = action.payload
572          space_id = p.get("space_id")
573          if space_id is None:
574              spaces = [s.id for s in spec.shared_spaces
575                        if actor in s.readers or actor in s.writers]
576          else:
577              spaces = [space_id]
578          after = p.get("after_sequence")
579          if after is None:
580              after = min((self.store.shared_cursor(self.session_id, actor, s) for s in spaces),
581                          default=0)
582          entries = self.store.shared_entries(self.session_id, spaces,
583                                              after_sequence=int(after),
584                                              limit=int(p.get("limit", 50)))
585          if advance and entries:
586              for s in spaces:
587                  seq = max((e.sequence for e in entries if e.space_id == s), default=0)
588                  if seq:
589                      self.store.advance_shared_cursor(self.session_id, actor, s, seq)
590          return Reduction(
591              events=[],
592              receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=action.kind,
593                              result={"entries": [e.model_dump(mode="json") for e in entries],
594                                      "next_sequence": entries[-1].sequence if entries else after}),
595          )
596  
597      def _apply_patch_action(self, action: TeamAction, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; Reduction:
598          p = action.payload
599          patch_id = p.get("patch_id")
600          if patch_id:
601              patch = self.store.get_patch(patch_id)
602              if p.get("reject"):
603                  self.store.set_patch_status(patch.patch_id, PatchStatus.REJECTED)
604                  return Reduction(
605                      events=[EventDraft(kind=EventKind.TOPOLOGY_REJECTED,
606                                         payload={"patch_id": patch.patch_id,
607                                                  "proposer": patch.proposer})],
608                      receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=action.kind,
609                                      result={"patch_id": patch.patch_id, "status": "REJECTED"}),
610                  )

314      def _reduce(self, action: TeamAction, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; Reduction:
315          kind = action.kind
316          actor = action.actor_id
317          p = action.payload
318  
319          match kind:
320              case ActionKind.USER_MESSAGE | ActionKind.USER_SUPPLEMENT:
321                  # user input resumes a paused session (plan section 9.3)
322                  session = self.store.get_session(self.session_id)
323                  if session is not None and SessionStatus(session["status"]) is SessionStatus.PAUSED:
324                      self.store.set_session_status(self.session_id, SessionStatus.ACTIVE)
325                  goal_id = self._ensure_goal(spec)
326                  text = str(p["text"])
327                  return Reduction(
328                      events=[EventDraft(
329                          kind=EventKind.USER_MESSAGE,
330                          payload={"text": text, "goal_id": goal_id,
331                                   "supplement": kind is ActionKind.USER_SUPPLEMENT,
332                                   "to": spec.leader_id},
333                          actor_id="user",
334                      )],
335                      receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,
336                                      result={"received": True, "goal_id": goal_id}),
337                  )
338  
339              case ActionKind.SEND_MESSAGE:
340                  target = p.get("target")
341                  targets = ([a.id for a in spec.agents if a.id != actor] if target == "*"
342                             else [target])
343                  targets = [t for t in targets if t != actor and spec.can_send(actor, t)]
344                  text = str(p.get("text", ""))
345                  return Reduction(
346                      events=[EventDraft(kind=EventKind.MESSAGE,
347                                         payload={"text": text, "from": actor, "target": t},

352  
353              case ActionKind.ASSIGN_TASK:

803          # keep only members whose identity, config or permissions actually changed
804          result: list[str] = []

814      @staticmethod
815      def _permissions_changed(old: TeamSpec, new: TeamSpec, agent_id: str) -&gt; bool:
816          def perms(spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; tuple:
817              sends = sorted(a.id for a in spec.agents if spec.can_send(agent_id, a.id))
818              delegates = sorted(a.id for a in spec.agents if spec.can_delegate(agent_id, a.id))
819              spaces = sorted(
820                  (s.id, agent_id in s.readers, agent_id in s.writers)
821                  for s in spec.shared_spaces
822              )
823              observers = sorted(
824                  (o.agent_id, tuple(o.subjects), tuple(sorted(o.event_types)))
825                  for o in spec.observers
826              )
827              return sends, delegates, spaces, observers
828  
829          return perms(old) != perms(new)
830  
831      def _sync_members(self, new: TeamSpec, old: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:
832          """Ensure runtime rows exist for new members; bump config revisions on change."""
833          for a in new.agents:
834              self.store.ensure_agent(self.session_id, a.id)
835          for a in new.agents:
836              old_a = next((x for x in old.agents if x.id == a.id), None)
837              if old_a is None or old_a != a:
838                  revision = self.store.bump_config_revision(self.session_id, a.id)
839                  if self.store.agent_status(self.session_id, a.id) is AgentStatus.DRAINING:
840                      self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, a.id, AgentStatus.IDLE)
841                  _ = revision
842          for a in old.agents:
843              if not any(x.id == a.id for x in new.agents):
844                  self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, a.id, AgentStatus.REMOVED)
845                  self._hand_over_removed_member(a.id, new)
846  
847      def _hand_over_removed_member(self, agent_id: str, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:
848          """A removed member's unfinished work goes back to the Leader for a new
849          decision; results and audit records are kept (plan section 8)."""
850          handed = self.store.reassign_tasks(
851              agent_id, spec.leader_id, [TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.BLOCKED])
852          for task_id in handed:
853              # a handed-over task must be announced to its new assignee
854              self.store.del_meta(f"task_ready_announced:{task_id}")
855          dropped = self.store.drop_pending_deliveries(
856              self.session_id, agent_id, "member removed before delivery")
857          action = TeamAction(action_id=f"member-removed:{agent_id}",
858                              session_id=self.session_id, actor_id="system",
859                              kind=ActionKind.APPLY_TOPOLOGY_PATCH, payload={})
860          self._persist_events(action, spec, [EventDraft(
861              kind=EventKind.MEMBER_REMOVED,
862              payload={"agent_id": agent_id, "hands_over_to": spec.leader_id,
863                       "handed_over_tasks": handed, "dropped_deliveries": dropped,
864                       "note": "results and work directories are kept for review"},
865          )])
866  
867      def _cancel_task(self, action: TeamAction, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; Reduction:
868          task = self.store.get_task(action.payload["task_id"])
869          if task.status in (TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED, TaskStatus.FAILED, TaskStatus.CANCELLED):
870              return Reduction(
871                  events=[],
872                  receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=action.kind,
873                                  result={"task_id": task.task_id, "status": task.status}),
874              )
875          active = self.store.active_run_for_agent(self.session_id, task.assignee)
876          events = [EventDraft(kind=EventKind.TASK_CANCELLED,
877                               payload={"task_id": task.task_id, "assignee": task.assignee,
878                                        "requester": task.requester,
879                                        "status": "CANCEL_REQUESTED"},
880                               task_id=task.task_id)]
881          if active is not None and (active.task_id == task.task_id or active.task_id is None):
882              self.store.set_run_cancel_requested(active.run_id)
883              self.store.set_task_cancel_requested(task.task_id)
884              status = "CANCEL_REQUESTED"
885          else:
886              self.store.compare_and_set_task(task.task_id, task.status, TaskStatus.CANCELLED)
887              status = "CANCELLED"
888          return Reduction(events=events,
889                           receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=action.kind,
890                                           result={"task_id": task.task_id, "status": status}))
891  
892      def _completion_blockers(self, spec: TeamSpec, current_run: str | None = None) -&gt; list[str]:
893          blockers: list[str] = []
894          live_runs = self.store.runs_for_session(
895              self.session_id,
896              [TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING, TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK,
897               TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL])
898          live_runs = [r for r in live_runs if r.run_id != current_run]
899          if live_runs:
900              blockers.append("active turns: " + ", ".join(
901                  f"{r.agent_id}:{r.status}" for r in live_runs))
902          unknown = self.store.runs_for_session(self.session_id, [TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN])
903          if unknown:
904              blockers.append("outcome-unknown operations: " + ", ".join(
905                  r.run_id for r in unknown))
906          un = self.store.tasks_for_session(
907              self.session_id,
908              [TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.RUNNING, TaskStatus.BLOCKED])
909          if un:
910              blockers.append("unfinished tasks: " + ", ".join(
911                  f"{t.task_id}:{t.status}" for t in un))
912          pend = self.store.pending_approvals(self.session_id)
913          pending_approvals = [a for a in pend if a is not None]
914          if pending_approvals:
915              blockers.append("pending approvals: " + ", ".join(
916                  a.approval_id for a in pending_approvals))
917          return blockers

920  
921      def _persist_events(self, action: TeamAction, spec: TeamSpec,
922                          drafts: list[EventDraft]) -&gt; None:
923          batch_by_agent: dict[str, int] = {}
924          for draft in drafts:
925              actor = draft.actor_id or action.actor_id
926              audience = (draft.audience if draft.audience is not None
927                          else views.event_audience(spec, draft.kind, actor, draft.payload,
928                                                    draft.targets))
929              push = views.event_push(spec, draft.kind, actor, draft.payload, draft.targets)
930              if draft.push is not None:
931                  # explicit recipients are additive; observers still get their subscription
932                  push = sorted(set(push) | set(draft.push))
933              event = TeamEvent(
934                  event_id=new_id("evt"),
935                  session_id=self.session_id,
936                  actor_id=actor,
937                  task_id=draft.task_id,
938                  kind=draft.kind,
939                  payload=draft.payload,
940                  audience=audience,
941                  topology_revision=self.store.current_revision(self.session_id),
942                  causation_id=action.action_id,
943              )
944              self.store.append_event(event)
945              for recipient in push:
946                  batch = batch_by_agent.get(recipient)
947                  if batch is None:
948                      batch = self.store.next_batch_no(self.session_id, recipient)
949                      batch_by_agent[recipient] = batch
950                  scope = views.observer_scope_for(spec, recipient, draft.kind, actor, draft.payload)

674      def _apply_operations(self, spec: TeamSpec, operations: list[dict]) -&gt; tuple[TeamSpec | None, str | None]:
675          data = spec.model_dump(mode="json")
676          cfg_tools = set(self.catalog.tools)
677          cfg_models = set(self.catalog.models)
678          for op in operations:
679              if not isinstance(op, dict):
680                  return None, f"operation must be a mapping, got {type(op).__name__}"
681              what = op.get("op")
682              match what:
683                  case "add_agent":
684                      agent = op.get("agent") or {}
685                      if any(a["id"] == agent.get("id") for a in data["agents"]):
686                          return None, f"member {agent.get('id')!r} already exists"
687                      if agent.get("model_profile") not in cfg_models:
688                          return None, (f"unknown model profile {agent.get('model_profile')!r}; "
689                                        "configure it in user config first")
690                      for tb in agent.get("tool_bindings") or []:
691                          if tb not in cfg_tools and tb not in BUILTIN_TOOL_BINDINGS:
692                              return None, f"unknown tool binding {tb!r}"
693                      data["agents"].append(agent)
694                      for ch in op.get("channels") or []:
695                          data["channels"].append(ch)
696                      for sp in op.get("shared_spaces") or []:
697                          existing = next((s for s in data["shared_spaces"]
698                                           if s["id"] == sp.get("id")), None)
699                          if existing:
700                              for key in ("readers", "writers"):
701                                  for who in sp.get(key) or []:
702                                      if who not in existing[key]:
703                                          existing[key].append(who)
704                          else:
705                              data["shared_spaces"].append(
706                                  {"id": sp.get("id"), "readers": sp.get("readers") or [],
707                                   "writers": sp.get("writers") or []})
708                  case "remove_agent":
709                      agent_id = op.get("agent_id")
710                      if agent_id == spec.leader_id:
711                          return None, "the Leader cannot be removed"
712                      if not any(a["id"] == agent_id for a in data["agents"]):
713                          return None, f"unknown member {agent_id!r}"
714                      data["agents"] = [a for a in data["agents"] if a["id"] != agent_id]
715                      data["channels"] = [c for c in data["channels"]
716                                          if c["source"] != agent_id
717                                          and agent_id not in c["targets"]]
718                      data["observers"] = [o for o in data["observers"]

 956      # ---------------------------------------------------------------- schedule

 958      def _schedule(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:
 959          """Create/wake runs for pending deliveries and ready tasks."""
 960          session = self.session_id
 961          self._apply_boundary_patches(spec)
 962          self._block_unrunnable_tasks(spec)
 963          self._announce_ready_tasks(spec)
 964          for agent in spec.agents:
 965              status = self.store.agent_status(session, agent.id)
 966              if status in (AgentStatus.REMOVED, AgentStatus.DRAINING):
 967                  continue
 968              pending = self.store.pending_deliveries(session, agent.id)
 969              if not pending:
 970                  continue
 971              waiting = self._waiting_run(agent.id)
 972              active = self._active_runs(agent.id)
 973              if active:
 974                  run = active[0]
 975                  fresh = [d for d in pending if d["delivery_id"] not in run.input_delivery_ids]
 976                  ids = [d["delivery_id"] for d in fresh]
 977                  if not ids:
 978                      continue
 979                  self.store.append_run_inputs(run.run_id, ids)
 980                  if run.status is TurnStatus.RUNNING:
 981                      self.mid_turn_pushes.append((run.run_id, [
 982                          {"kind": d["event_kind"], "from": d["event_actor"],
 983                           "task_id": d["event_task_id"],
 984                           "payload": json.loads(d["payload_json"])}
 985                          for d in fresh
 986                      ]))
 987                  continue
 988              if waiting is not None:
 989                  if waiting.status is TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL:
 990                      continue
 991                  # ponytail: a waiting turn resumes when ALL waited tasks are
 992                  # terminal, or on user input / cancel. Partial results stay
 993                  # queued; split waits into separate waits if partial wake needed.
 994                  pending_wait = [t for t in waiting.waiting_on
 995                                  if (task := self.store.get_task(t)) is not None
 996                                  and task.status not in (TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED, TaskStatus.FAILED,
 997                                                          TaskStatus.CANCELLED)]
 998                  user_input = any(d["event_kind"] == EventKind.USER_MESSAGE for d in pending)
 999                  if pending_wait and not user_input and not waiting.cancel_requested:
1000                      continue
1001                  if self.store.update_run_status_where(
1002                          waiting.run_id, TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK, TurnStatus.RUNNING):
1003                      self.store.append_run_inputs(
1004                          waiting.run_id, [d["delivery_id"] for d in pending])
1005                  continue
1006              if not self._turn_budget_ok():
1007                  self._emit_limit_reached()
1008                  continue
1009              task = self._next_ready_task(agent.id)
1010              run = TurnRun(
1011                  run_id=new_id("run"),
1012                  session_id=session,
1013                  task_id=task.task_id if task else None,
1014                  goal_id=self._current_goal_id(),
1015                  agent_id=agent.id,
1016                  config_revision=self.store.agent_config_revision(session, agent.id),
1017                  topology_revision=self.store.current_revision(session),
1018                  status=TurnStatus.QUEUED,
1019                  input_delivery_ids=[d["delivery_id"] for d in pending],
1020                  context_ref=self._context_ref(agent.id),
1021              )
1022              self.store.insert_run(run)
1023  
1024      def _block_unrunnable_tasks(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:
1025          """Dependencies that failed/cancelled make dependents BLOCKED, never silently stuck."""
1026          for task in self.store.tasks_for_session(self.session_id, [TaskStatus.PENDING]):
1027              deps = [self.store.get_task(d) for d in task.dependencies]
1028              broken = [d for d in deps
1029                        if d is not None and d.status in (TaskStatus.FAILED, TaskStatus.CANCELLED)]
1030              if not broken:
1031                  continue
1032              if self.store.compare_and_set_task(task.task_id, TaskStatus.PENDING,
1033                                                 TaskStatus.BLOCKED):
1034                  draft = EventDraft(
1035                      kind=EventKind.TASK_BLOCKED,
1036                      payload={"task_id": task.task_id, "assignee": task.assignee,
1037                               "requester": task.requester,
1038                               "reason": "dependency " + ", ".join(
1039                                   f"{d.task_id}:{d.status}" for d in broken)},
1040                      task_id=task.task_id,
1041                  )
1042                  action = TeamAction(action_id=f"blocked:{task.task_id}",
1043                                      session_id=self.session_id, actor_id="system",
1044                                      kind=ActionKind.CANCEL_TASK, payload={"task_id": task.task_id})
1045                  self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])
1046  
1047      def _announce_ready_tasks(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:
1048          """One TASK_READY ping per task, once its dependencies are satisfied."""
1049          for task in sorted(self.store.tasks_for_session(self.session_id, [TaskStatus.PENDING]),
1050                             key=lambda t: t.created_at):
1051              deps = [self.store.get_task(d) for d in task.dependencies]
1052              if not all(d is not None and d.status is TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED for d in deps):
1053                  continue
1054              if self.store.get_meta(f"task_ready_announced:{task.task_id}"):
1055                  continue
1056              draft = EventDraft(
1057                  kind=EventKind.TASK_READY,
1058                  payload={"task_id": task.task_id, "assignee": task.assignee,
1059                           "requester": task.requester,
1060                           "description": task.description, "acceptance": task.acceptance},
1061                  task_id=task.task_id,
1062              )
1063              action = TeamAction(action_id=f"ready:{task.task_id}", session_id=self.session_id,
1064                                  actor_id="system", kind=ActionKind.CANCEL_TASK, payload={})
1065              self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])
1066              self.store.set_meta(f"task_ready_announced:{task.task_id}", "1")
1067  
1068      def _apply_boundary_patches(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:
1069          for patch in self.store.patches_in_status(self.session_id, PatchStatus.WAITING_BOUNDARY):
1070              if any(self._agent_has_live_run(a) for a in patch.affected_agents):
1071                  continue
1072              current = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)
1073              new_spec, error = self._apply_operations(current, patch.operations)
1074              if error:
1075                  self.store.set_patch_status(patch.patch_id, PatchStatus.FAILED)
1076                  continue
1077              revision = self.store.save_team_spec(self.session_id, new_spec)
1078              self.store.set_patch_status(patch.patch_id, PatchStatus.APPLIED)
1079              self._sync_members(new_spec, current)
1080              self._persist_patch_event(patch, revision)

1082      def _persist_patch_event(self, patch: TopologyPatch, revision: int) -&gt; None:
1083          spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)
1084          draft = EventDraft(kind=EventKind.TOPOLOGY_APPLIED,
1085                             payload={"patch_id": patch.patch_id, "revision": revision,
1086                                      "decided_by": patch.decided_by,
1087                                      "operations": patch.operations})
1088          action = TeamAction(action_id=f"patch-apply:{patch.patch_id}",
1089                              session_id=self.session_id, actor_id=patch.decided_by or patch.proposer,
1090                              kind=ActionKind.APPLY_TOPOLOGY_PATCH, payload={})
1091          self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])
1092  
1093      def _emit_limit_reached(self) -&gt; None:
1094          key = f"limit_notified:{self._current_goal_id()}"
1095          if self.store.get_meta(key):
1096              return
1097          self.store.set_meta(key, "1")
1098          spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)
1099          draft = EventDraft(kind=EventKind.LIMIT_REACHED,
1100                             payload={"kind": "max_turns_per_goal",
1101                                      "limit": spec.limits.max_turns_per_goal,
1102                                      "goal_id": self._current_goal_id()})
1103          action = TeamAction(action_id=new_id("sys"), session_id=self.session_id,
1104                              actor_id="system", kind=ActionKind.PAUSE_SESSION, payload={})
1105          self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])
1106  
1107      # ------------------------------------------------------------- small helpers
1108  
1109      def _agent_has_live_run(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; bool:
1110          return any(r.agent_id == agent_id for r in self.store.runs_for_session(
1111              self.session_id,
1112              [TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING, TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK,
1113               TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL]))
1114  
1115      def _active_runs(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; list[TurnRun]:
1116          return [r for r in self.store.runs_for_session(
1117              self.session_id, [TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING])
1118              if r.agent_id == agent_id]
1119  
1120      def _waiting_run(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; TurnRun | None:
1121          """The member's parked turn (waiting on tasks or on an approval)."""
1122          for r in self.store.runs_for_session(
1123                  self.session_id, [TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK, TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL]):
1124              if r.agent_id == agent_id:
1125                  return r
1126          return None
1127  
1128      def _next_ready_task(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; Task | None:
1129          for task in sorted(self.store.tasks_for_session(self.session_id, [TaskStatus.PENDING]),
1130                             key=lambda t: t.created_at):
1131              if task.assignee != agent_id:
1132                  continue
1133              deps = [self.store.get_task(d) for d in task.dependencies]
1134              if all(d is not None and d.status is TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED for d in deps):
1135                  return task
1136          return None
1137  
1138      def _turn_budget_ok(self) -&gt; bool:
1139          goal_id = self._current_goal_id()
1140          if not goal_id:
1141              return True
1142          spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)
1143          used = self.store.count_goal_runs(self.session_id, goal_id)
1144          return used &lt; spec.limits.max_turns_per_goal
1145  
1146      def _task_result(self, task_id: str) -&gt; dict:
1147          task = self.store.get_task(task_id)
1148          return {"task_id": task_id, "status": task.status if task else "UNKNOWN",
1149                  "result_refs": task.result_refs if task else []}
1150  
1151      def _context_ref(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; str:
1152          epoch = self.store.agent_context_epoch(self.session_id, agent_id)
1153          return f"ctx:{agent_id}:{epoch}"
1154  
1155      def _ensure_goal(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; str:
1156          session = self.store.get_session(self.session_id)
1157          goal_id = session["goal_id"] if session else None
1158          if goal_id and session["goal_state"] in ("active", "done"):
1159              if session["goal_state"] == "done":
1160                  goal_id = new_id("goal")
1161                  self.store.set_goal_state(self.session_id, goal_id, "active")
1162              return goal_id
1163          goal_id = goal_id or new_id("goal")
1164          self.store.set_goal_state(self.session_id, goal_id, "active")
1165          return goal_id
1166  
1167      def _current_goal_id(self) -&gt; str | None:
1168          session = self.store.get_session(self.session_id)
1169          return session["goal_id"] if session else None
1170  
1171      def _wake_approval_run(self, run_id: str) -&gt; None:
1172          run = self.store.get_run(run_id)
1173          if run is not None and run.status is TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL:
1174              self.store.update_run_status_where(
1175                  run_id, TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL, TurnStatus.RUNNING)</message>

Control.submit 是否真正单事务：动作回执去重（action_id；重复动作返回原回执）、事件、任务/共享空间变更、QUEUED 执行意图是否同一事务提交；有无先持久化意图后写业务状态的窗口。\n2. 崩溃窗口（§9.2/T8/T21）：动作提交后回执丢失、事件已写但投递未确认、回合中途死亡等路径；恢复核对逻辑是否存在「消费位置先推进、后确认」类错误顺序；重复投递是否可能重复注入上下文。\n3. 投递批次号与 context_epoch（§7/T24）：新会话隔离、同名新成员不继承旧身份的实现是否完备；事件受众/版本快照语义（新增成员/通道不补发历史）。\n4. 拓扑 patch（§8/T11-T13）：base_revision 冲突、受影响集合计算、DRAINING 与边界等待、原子应用（不半应用）、权限上限校验、并发 patch 竞态；新增成员是否能在 Leader 同一回合内立即被委派（无死锁）。\n5. 资源上限（§6.4/T22）：max_turns_per_goal 计数在崩溃恢复后是否重复计数、LIMIT_REACHED 是否可能被冒充为完成、并发计数是否准确。\n6. 状态机合法性（§5.3）：任务/回合/目标/团队状态转换是否存在非法路径（完成与取消竞态、结果不明处理）；失败状态是否可能被静默吞掉。\n7. models.py：TeamSpec/动作/事件的校验完备性（未知字段、非法引用、ACL 一致性、观察者能力）；序列化/反序列化一致性。\n8. SQLite 实现细节：WAL/busy_timeout/事务边界/索引/并发写；JSON 字段解析容错；长事务风险。\n\n方法：通读实现后，对可疑点用 grep 追调用点、写最小复现脚本到 review/tmp/（不得改仓库源码）或运行相关确定性测试。每条发现给 file:line、影响、建议修复、严重度（P0-P3）。宁缺毋滥，禁止臆测；未能验证的标注「待验证」。\n\n验收标准：review/findings-core.md 按协议结构完整；每条发现可核验；明确列出未验证项；向 leader 发消息（一句话结论+Top5 发现+路径）；不修改任何被审文件（可用 git status 自证）。", "acceptance": "", "status": "BLOCKED", "requester": "leader"}, {"task_id": "task_d207400d534b", "description": "TeamAgents 代码审查 · 核心状态与持久化域（重派；上一轮被中断，无产出）。工作目录=仓库根。先读 review/PROTOCOL.md 并严格遵守（只读审查；只能在 review/** 下写文件）。\n\n【重要·写入策略】上一轮因单回合模型步数上限中断，本轮请尽早落盘：读完 control.py 与 models.py 后先把 findings 文件骨架 + 已确认发现写入 review/findings-core.md，随后每完成一节就追加更新，避免再次中断丢失进展。\n\n审查对象：src/teamagents/control.py、storage.py、models.py（全量精读）。参照：tests/test_p1_guards.py、test_p2_recovery.py、test_p2_cancel_pause.py、test_p4_topology.py、conftest.py。基准：方案 §2.2、§4、§5.3、§6.4、§7、§8、§9、§16-17（T1-T6/T8/T10-T14/T21/T22/T24）；docs/DECISIONS.md（D-1）。\n\n重点：\n1. `docs/DECISIONS.md`（D-6 `--team`、D-9 会话面板）、`README.md`、`docs/USER-GUIDE.md`。\n&gt; 审查对象：`src/teamagents/views.py`、`config.py`、`cli.py`、`tui/app.py`、`tui/panels.py`、`tui/approvals.py`、`tui/theme.py`（全量精读）。\n\n---\n\n## 一、范围与方法\n\n**精读文件（含行数）**\n\n| 文件 | 行数 | 说明 |\n|---|---|---|\n| `src/teamagents/views.py` | 205 | audience/push 两层信息权限、AgentView 构建 |\n| `src/teamagents/config.py` | 109 | 用户/项目配置合并、TeamSpec 导入导出、XDG |\n| `src/teamagents/cli.py` | 309 | TUI/--plain/doctor/validate/sessions/version |\n| `src/teamagents/tui/app.py` | 531 | 主界面、事件循环、会话管理 |\n| `src/teamagents/tui/panels.py` | 393 | ChatLog/PromptInput/状态栏/团队/任务/共享/日志/设置/会话面板 |\n| `src/teamagents/tui/approvals.py` | 81 | 批准队列（a/s/d） |\n| `src/teamagents/tui/theme.py` | 43 | Codex 配色 |\n\n**交叉阅读（用于判定语义是否正确，不作为审查对象）**：`models.py`、`storage.py`（events/deliveries/shared_cursor）、`control.py`（`_persist_events` 的投递创建、`_validate` 动作校验、`_read_shared`、dedupe）、`runtime.py`（`_execute_inner` 构建 view、`_finalize`、`_repl` 相关）、`session.py`、`sessions.py`、`providers.py`、`tools.py`、`execution.py`（仅用于比对 doctor 探针）、`tests/conftest.py`、`tests/test_config_cli.py`、`tests/test_p6_tui.py`、`tests/test_p6_sessions_ui.py`、`tests/test_t4_observer.py`、`tests/test_t6_isolation.py`、`docs/USER-GUIDE.md`、`README.md`、`docs/DECISIONS.md`。\n\n**方法**：先通读→对照方案/文档→对可疑点写最小复现（脚本整理在 `review/tmp/repro_surface.py`，见 §六 说明的挂载限制）→运行确定性测试。所有结论都有 `file:line` + 可重跑命令或实验输出；推测项在 §五 标注「待验证」。\n\n**确定性测试**（沙箱，未联网、无密钥）：\n\n```bash\n.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_config_cli.py tests/test_t4_observer.py \\\n  tests/test_t6_isolation.py tests/test_p6_tui.py tests/test_p6_sessions_ui.py -q\n# -&gt; <message type="tool">  1  # 实施决策记录（偏离与澄清）
  2  
  3  基准文档：`TeamAgents-Implementation-Plan.zh-CN.md`。**任何偏离方案的做法，先与用户确认再实现。**
  4  本文件只记录已确认的决策；未确认的候选方案写在对话里，不写进代码。
  5  
  6  ## D-1 短控制图：事务化步骤管线，不套 LangGraph StateGraph ✅（用户已确认 2026-09-11）
  7  
  8  - 方案原文：§2.1「LangGraph 负责……短流程团队控制图的执行」，§4 控制图执行
  9    `ingest → validate → reduce → schedule → persist → END`。
 10  - 实施：同样一组固定步骤实现在 `control.py::Control.submit`，整步在**一个 SQLite 事务**内提交
 11    （动作回执 + 事件 + 任务/共享空间变更 + 排队中的执行意图），执行意图持久化为 `QUEUED` TurnRun 后再交给运行时。
 12  - 理由：该管线不调用模型、无图状态需要检查点；业务数据库是权威，事务原子性强于图检查点恢复。
 13    套一层 StateGraph 只增加壳，不增加能力（DP-1 的实质要求「固定小图 + 拓扑是数据」已满足）。
 14  - 代价与升级路径：控制流步骤无法像图节点那样逐节点重放；若将来控制流出现跨事务的长时间步骤
 15    （例如需要中途调用外部系统），再引入 LangGraph 并按节点切分。
 16  
 17  ## D-2 工具权限：统一网关 + 逐调用批准 ✅（按方案 §12.2 执行，非偏离）
 18  
 19  Deep Agents 的 `permissions` 只覆盖其内置文件工具，不约束 Shell/MCP；因此
 20  `ToolGateway`（`agents.py`）是所有成员工具调用的唯一入口：团队动作（ACL 校验）与执行工具（权限评估 + 批准）
 21  共用同一路径，身份由运行时注入。此为实现细节，与方案一致。
 22  
 23  ## D-3 Codex 协议 Schema 不入库 ✅（用户已确认 2026-09-11）
 24  
 25  `teamagents doctor` 用本机 `codex app-server generate-json-schema` 现场生成并校验所需方法集合，
 26  避免 4.3MB 生成物随 CLI 升级漂移。方案要求「Schema 根据选定 CLI 版本生成」，此实现满足该要求。
 27  
 28  ## D-4 Codex 成员默认配置 ✅（用户已确认 2026-09-11）
 29  
 30  - 默认模型：不传 `model`，继承本机 `~/.codex/config.toml` 的默认模型与认证（当前为
 31    `gpt-6-astra`，走 apexin 中转）；成员级可在 TeamSpec/用户配置中覆盖 model 与工作目录。
 32  - 思考强度：**固定 `xhigh`**；若所选模型不支持 `xhigh`，自动回退 `max` 并记住结果（一次性校准）。
 33    每个回合通过 `turn/start` 的 `effort` 参数显式传入，不依赖本机默认值。
 34  - 沙箱/批准：每回合显式传 `sandbox`/`approvalPolicy`/`approvalsReviewer="user"`，
 35    不沿用本机 `auto_review` 的宽松设置（P0 已实测本机配置会放行 /tmp 写入）。
 36  
 37  ## D-5 网页搜索：AnySearch 走 HTTP 直连工具 ✅（用户已确认 2026-09-11）
 38  
 39  - 用户选择 (a)：接入 AnySearch。实测其接口为 `POST https://api.anysearch.com/v1/search`
 40    （Bearer `ANYSEARCH_API_KEY`，body `{"query","max_results"}`，返回 `code=0/data.results[]`），
 41    **不是 MCP 服务**。
 42  - 实施：`tools.py` 提供 `kind="web_search"`（provider=`anysearch`）与 `kind="web_fetch"`
 43    的内置工具；MCP 通用路径保留给其他搜索/提取服务（如 Tavily MCP）。工具是否出现由用户配置的
 44    绑定决定，绑定即授权（无逐次批准）。抓取工具带 SSRF 防护（禁内网/回环/保留地址）与体积上限。
