<message type="tool"> 45  ### 2.2 核心约束
 46  
 47  1. **团队结构是经过校验的数据。** 成员、通道、观察和共享权限由 TeamSpec 表达，模型生成的 Python 代码不能替代团队定义。
 48  2. **Leader 始终存在。** Leader 是用户的自然语言入口和团队变更决策者；单 Leader 团队必须完整运行。
 49  3. **成员有独立身份和上下文。** 私有子代理不自动成为团队成员；Leader 的管理权限不自动赋予其读取成员私有历史的权限。
 50  4. **信息权限在运行时执行。** 发送、观察、共享空间读写和工具权限分别校验，不能只靠提示词约定。
 51  5. **成员独立推进。** 同一成员同时最多执行一个活动回合，Leader 的交互不等待所有工作成员结束；变更在受影响成员的安全边界生效。
 52  6. **状态有明确权威来源。** TeamState 是紧凑运行视图；团队事实与动作回执以业务事务为准，成员执行检查点由 LangGraph 或 Codex 管理。
 53  7. **模型配置与拓扑分离。** 成员引用逻辑模型配置，供应商和凭据变化不改变其团队通信权限。
 54  8. **执行有边界、结果可追溯。** 任务完成、失败、取消和结果不明分别记录；资源超限不能冒充成功，取消不能冒充副作用回滚。
 55  
 56  ## 3. 技术选型与架构决策
 57  
 58  采用 Python 3.12+、Deep Agents、LangGraph、Pydantic、Textual、SQLite 和 asyncio。依赖用 `pyproject.toml` 管理并生成锁文件。Python SDK 是内部可测试的核心接口，首要产品入口是 TUI。
 59  
 60  本文使用三组稳定编号：`DP-1`～`DP-12` 表示下表完整定义的架构决策，`P0`～`P7` 表示第 16 节的开发阶段，`T1`～`T24` 表示第 17 节的验收场景。外部官方文档链接用于核对依赖接口，产品要求和实现约束以本文为准。
 61  
 62  | 决策 | 首版选择 | 实施含义 |
 63  |---|---|---|
 64  | DP-1 执行结构 | 通用团队控制图 | 固定小图处理团队事务；拓扑是数据，不按团队形状生成代码 |
 65  | DP-2 调度 | 混合 | Leader 负责语义决策；代码负责就绪队列、并发限额、权限和终止条件 |
 66  | DP-3 通信 | 持久化事件 + 收件视图 | 消息与任务形成事件；按权限、收件人和消费位置构造增量视图 |
 67  | DP-4 共享空间 | 追加式结构化记录 | SQLite 保存短条目及附件引用；大文件保存在工作目录或制品目录 |
 68  | DP-5 私有上下文 | 每成员独立持久化线程 | 按会话、成员实例和上下文代次区分；不依赖动态节点调用顺序 |
 69  | DP-6 Deep Agent 生命周期 | 配置版本内复用 | 成员配置稳定时复用已创建实例；配置变更在边界后重建 |
 70  | DP-7 模型 | 逻辑配置名 | 团队引用模型 profile；供应商、API 地址、密钥引用在部署配置中 |
 71  | DP-8 用户交互 | Leader 固定入口 | 所有自然语言输入交给 Leader；TUI 控制操作走运行时命令 |
 72  | DP-9 观察者 | 显式事件订阅和能力 | 观察权不等于发送、停止或修改团队的权限 |
 73  | DP-10 动态拓扑 | 带版本的结构化 patch | Leader 提交，代码验证，在受影响成员的安全边界原子切换 |
 74  | DP-11 并行执行 | asyncio 管理独立成员执行 | 每个内置成员仍在自己的 LangGraph 中执行；不设置全队完成屏障 |
 75  | DP-12 完成判断 | Leader 判断 + 硬约束 | Leader 声明目标完成；运行时检查必要任务、失败项和资源上限 |
 76  
 77  Textual 负责视图、键盘输入和后台工作消息；团队逻辑不能放进 Widget。其 Worker 机制支持将耗时操作与界面更新分离。[Textual Workers](https://textual.textualize.io/guide/workers/)
 78  
 79  P0 必须记录并验证 Python、Deep Agents、LangGraph、模型集成包、Textual 和 Codex CLI 的具体版本，产出依赖锁文件及兼容性记录。Codex 协议 Schema 根据选定 CLI 版本生成；依赖升级后重新运行受影响的接口测试。
 80  
 81  ## 4. 总体结构与执行模型
 82  
 83  ```mermaid
 84  flowchart TD
 85      U[用户] --&gt; UI[Textual TUI]
 86      UI --&gt; R[Session Runtime]
 87      R --&gt; C[短流程 Team Control Graph]
 88      C --&gt; DB[(SQLite 团队事务与事件)]
 89      R --&gt; L[Leader / Deep Agents]
 90      R --&gt; A[其他内置成员 / Deep Agents]
 91      R --&gt; X[Codex Adapter / 本机 App Server]
 92      L --&gt; CP[(LangGraph 成员检查点)]
 93      A --&gt; CP
 94      X --&gt; CX[(Codex 私有会话)]
 95      L --&gt; C
 96      A --&gt; C
 97      X --&gt; R
 98      R --&gt; UI
 99  ```
100  
101  团队控制图仅执行 `ingest → validate → reduce → schedule → persist → END`。它不等待模型生成、不等待 Shell 完成、不调用正在执行的成员来获取答案。每次短流程将任务启动、取消、通知等执行意图与业务状态一起提交，之后才交给执行驱动处理。
102  
103  Session Runtime 持有成员执行句柄，通过 asyncio 启动和管理这些意图。每个内置成员运行自己的持久化 Deep Agents 图；Codex 运行在其自身会话中。成员结果和工具发起的团队动作返回控制图处理。
104  
105  采用这一结构的原因：LangGraph 同一个执行步骤中的节点并行运行，但状态在步骤完成后更新。将所有长回合放进同一 fan-out 批次，会妨碍新消息和成员级变更及时生效。这里保留 LangGraph 的个体执行和短控制流程，将成员间的唤醒与并行交给 TeamAgents 的团队调度。[LangGraph runtime](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/pregel)
106  
107  运行约束：
108  
109  - 每个会话只有一个团队事务写入入口；多个会话可分别运行。
110  - 同一成员同时最多一个活动回合；等待批准或等待任务不占用执行槽位。
111  - Leader 保留独立执行额度，避免工作成员占满并发容量。
112  - 成员崩溃只改变相关执行状态，不通过共享 `TaskGroup` 的失败传播取消整队。
113  - 内存队列只负责唤醒；待办工作、执行意图和消息是否处理必须有持久化记录。
114  
115  ## 5. 会话、任务与回合
116  
117  ### 5.1 身份与配置
118  
119  核心术语定义如下：
120  
121  | 术语 | 含义 |
122  |---|---|
123  | Session（会话） | 一段可持续对话和恢复的运行记录，是持久化、团队复用和默认信息隔离的单位 |
124  | Team（团队） | TeamSpec 在会话中的运行实例；每个会话维护一个团队，其成员与拓扑可随版本变更 |
125  | TeamAgent（团队成员） | 有稳定身份、角色和执行上下文的参与者；可为内置 Deep Agents 成员或外部 Codex 执行成员 |
126  | Leader | 每个团队唯一的用户交互与团队变更决策角色，由内置 Deep Agents 成员承担 |
127  | 私有子代理 | 在某个成员内部执行的辅助 Agent，由该成员的执行后端管理，不自动获得独立团队身份或权限 |
128  | 拓扑 | 成员及其通信、委派、观察和共享空间访问关系 |
129  | SharedSpace（共享空间） | 具有独立读写权限的团队知识区域，保存结构化条目和成果引用 |
130  | TeamState | 控制图处理事件时使用的紧凑团队状态视图，包含成员状态、待办引用、调度与完成状态 |
131  
132  会话中只有一个活动 Leader；Leader 可独立执行，也可建立其他成员。内置成员和 Codex 的具体能力差异见第 10 节。
133  
134  `agent_id` 为稳定内部身份，显示名称单独保存。删除后用同名创建的成员使用新 ID，不能自动继承被删除成员的上下文。每个成员保存 `config_revision` 和 `context_epoch`；内置成员线程键由 `session_id + agent_id + context_epoch` 确定。
135  
136  更换模型时尽量保留同一成员的标准化对话；供应商专有块不能直接跨协议发送。若需新上下文代次，使用该成员自己的交接摘要续接，旧记录仍保留；这不使私有内容进入 Leader 的视图。
137  
138  ### 5.2 关键对象
139  
140  | 对象 | 必要字段 |
141  |---|---|
142  | TeamSpec | `schema_version, leader_id, agents, channels, observers, shared_spaces, limits` |
143  | TeamState | `session_id, team_spec_revision, agent_status, pending_work_refs, scheduler_state, completion_state, event_cursor` |
144  | AgentSpec | `id, name, role, runtime_kind, instructions, model_profile, tool_bindings, skills, workspace_policy` |
145  | ChannelSpec | `source, targets, mode`；mode 为 message/task/broadcast |
146  | ObserverSpec | `agent_id, subjects, event_types, payload_scope, wake_policy, capabilities` |
147  | Task | `task_id, parent_task_id, requester, assignee, description, acceptance, dependencies, status, result_refs` |
148  | TurnRun | `run_id, task_id, agent_id, config_revision, topology_revision, status, input_delivery_ids, context_ref, external_turn_id` |
149  | TeamAction | `action_id, session_id, actor_id, run_id, kind, payload` |
150  | TeamEvent | `event_id, sequence, session_id, actor_id, task_id, kind, payload, audience, topology_revision, causation_id` |
151  | TopologyPatch | `patch_id, base_revision, proposer, decided_by, operations, affected_agents, status` |
152  | ApprovalRequest | `approval_id, agent_id, run_id, tool_call_id, operation_hash, requested_scope, policy_revision, status` |
153  | SharedEntry | `entry_id, space_id, author, kind, content/ref, supersedes, sequence` |
154  | AgentView | `assignment, inbox_delta, permitted_shared_delta, relevant_topology, capabilities, delivery_ids` |
155  
156  `actor_id`、会话和运行身份由运行时注入，不能信任模型传入的身份字段。所有外部配置和动作使用严格类型校验；未知字段、未知引用和不支持的枚举值均返回可理解的错误。
157  
158  验证至少覆盖：Leader 唯一且存在；成员 ID 唯一；模型、工具、通道、观察和共享空间引用有效；任务依赖无环；权限不超过用户配置；外部 Codex 成员只能由 Leader 委派；通信环路有执行上限；配置限制为有效正数。成员私有上下文引用不能作为普通共享附件发布。
159  
160  ### 5.3 状态机
161  
162  - 会话：`ACTIVE → PAUSED / IDLE / CLOSED`；重新打开已有会话恢复其记录。
163  - 任务：`PENDING → RUNNING → SUCCEEDED / FAILED / CANCELLED`；可进入 `BLOCKED` 等待依赖、用户信息或恢复核对。
164  - 回合：`QUEUED → RUNNING → WAITING_TASK / WAITING_APPROVAL / COMPLETED / FAILED / CANCELLED / OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`。
165  - 成员：`IDLE / BUSY / WAITING / DRAINING / REMOVED`；任务完成不等于成员被删除。
166  - 变更：`PROPOSED → ACCEPTED → WAITING_BOUNDARY → APPLIED`，或 `REJECTED / FAILED`。
167  
168  `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN` 表示外部操作可能发生但结果无法确认。它不能被标成成功，也不能直接变成可自动重试。
169  
170  一个回合从接受一次调度输入开始，覆盖该成员为当前输入进行的模型和工具调用，直至完成、失败或取消。持久化等待可跨多次底层 `ainvoke` 恢复，仍属于同一 `run_id`。
171  
172  ## 6. Leader、组队与调度
173  
174  ### 6.1 自然语言建立团队

[Read 130 lines (lines 45-174 of 565 total). 391 lines remaining from offset 174.]</message>
<message type="tool">206  - 取消可立即提出请求，但要等待执行停止的确认；不可中断的外部操作可能导致延迟或结果不明，TUI 如实显示。
207  
208  ### 6.4 完成与资源约束
209  
210  Leader 通过 `signal_done` 给出结果和完成说明。运行时只有在必要任务成功或被明确取消、相关工作回合均已结束、没有未解决的批准和结果不明操作时，才接受目标完成申请；Leader 当前回合正常结束后提交目标完成事件。界面保留团队以便继续会话。
211  
212  以下是可调整的初始工程默认值，均针对单个目标或单次调用，不限制会话总寿命：
213  
214  | 限制 | 建议默认值 |
215  |---|---|
216  | 活动工作成员并发 | 4，另给 Leader 保留 1 个执行额度 |
217  | 团队成员数 | 16，包含 Leader |
218  | 单目标回合启动上限 | 200；并行回合分别计数，崩溃后恢复原回合不重复计数 |
219  | 单回合模型步骤 | 50 |
220  | 单次模型请求超时 | 120 秒，可按模型覆盖 |
221  | 单回合活动时间 | 15 分钟；显式等待时间不计入 |
222  | 自动瞬时错误重试 | 最多 2 次，退避并尊重供应商限流响应 |
223  
224  达到上限时停止派发新工作、保存状态，向 Leader/用户报告 `LIMIT_REACHED`，允许用户调整后继续；不得冒充完成。个体私有子任务也必须计入所属成员的资源使用。
225  
226  ## 7. 团队动作、通信与信息边界
227  
228  内置成员按权限获得以下动作：
229  
230  | 动作 | 合约 |
231  |---|---|
232  | `send_message` | 仅向允许的目标发送；广播也逐目标验证 |
233  | `assign_task` | 创建任务与依赖，返回任务 ID；不阻塞等待执行 |
234  | `complete_task` | 仅当前被分配者提交，检查任务状态和输出引用 |
235  | `wait_for_tasks` | 登记唤醒条件，释放执行额度，等待结果或用户输入 |
236  | `publish_shared` | 向有写权限的共享空间追加条目 |
237  | `read_shared / list_shared` | 按读权限及分页获取；不返回未获授权空间的内容 |
238  | `request_help` | 向 Leader 发送当前任务的帮助请求 |
239  | `propose_team_change` | 提交建议给 Leader，不能直接修改团队 |
240  | `apply_topology_patch` | 仅 Leader 可调用，提交经版本和权限校验的变更 |
241  | `signal_done` | 仅 Leader 可声明当前用户目标完成 |
242  
243  团队动作在调用时校验并提交，成功回执意味着业务事务已经提交。同一回合中后续 `read_shared` 能看到已提交条目；并行调用之间只保证实际提交顺序，不推断模型想要的顺序。
244  
245  动作提交使用稳定 `action_id`，推荐从持久化 `run_id + tool_call_id` 派生。控制图将动作回执、事件和任务/共享空间变更放入一个 SQLite 事务；重复动作返回原回执。参数相同但动作 ID 不同不自动视为重复。
246  
247  每条消息有固定收件人和事件身份。建立 AgentView 时只取该成员有权接收且尚未投递的内容。投递采用至少一次传输，配合稳定消息 ID 和检查点确认避免上下文重复注入；不能在仅放入内存队列后就推进消费位置。
248  
249  每个成员使用单调递增的投递批次号；新消息与“最近已应用批次号”一起保存到其 LangGraph 检查点。确认回执丢失时先查该标记，再决定是否重送；不能依赖可能被上下文整理移除的历史消息来判断。团队收件投影只在该检查点得到确认后推进。该机制只处理投递元数据，不由 TeamAgents 接管个体对话管理。
250  
251  观察者规则明确列出对象、事件类型及载荷范围，例如只看状态、看获准公开的消息、看结果。观察权不授予成员私有对话读取权；观察事件也不会自动生成可回信通道。需要请求修订或停止时，按独立能力处理；团队结构变更仍由 Leader 决策。
252  
253  已有事件保存发送时的受众和版本；增加成员或通道不自动补发全部历史。尚未进入上下文的消息在投递时还须满足当前授权；撤销权限不可能删除模型已经接收的信息。
254  
255  用户作为本地操作者可在 TUI 查阅已记录的成员对话、工具活动和事件；这一读取路径不会把记录注入 Leader。只展示实际记录到的内容，不承诺供应商未提供的内部推理。
256  
257  ## 8. 动态拓扑变更
258  
259  支持 `add_agent`、`remove_agent`、修改角色/指令/模型/工具、增删通信通道、调整观察规则、调整共享空间权限。不能删除唯一 Leader，也不能由模型自行扩大用户授予的工具权限。
260  
261  处理流程：
262  
263  1. 成员提议或用户向 Leader 提出要求；Leader 可以接受、修改或拒绝。
264  2. Leader 提交含 `base_revision` 的 patch；代码检查角色、引用、资源限额、模型可用性和权限上限。
265  3. 对需要变更的活动成员标记 `DRAINING`，不再为其启动新回合；不受影响的成员继续执行。
266  4. 等受影响回合终结，或显式取消并确认停止。单纯 WAITING_APPROVAL 不算回合结束。
267  5. 按最新状态再次校验；在一个事务中更新 TeamSpec 版本、任务归属及事件，然后重建必要的成员配置。
268  6. 版本过期则返回冲突给 Leader 重做决定，不做无依据的自动覆盖。
269  
270  新增空闲成员可在下一次控制图提交边界生效，不需要等待全队。跨多个成员的 patch 等所有相关成员满足条件后一次应用，不能半改角色、半改通信权限。
271  
272  受影响集合按操作语义计算：删除、角色/模型/工具变更及撤销权限需要等待对应活动回合；纯新增成员和不会使现有执行失效的通道授权可直接在短控制图边界提交。因此 Leader 可以在同一回合创建成员、收到成功回执后委派任务，不会等待自己结束而死锁。动作授权查询当前已提交版本，回合启动版本用于审计；尚未生效的 patch 不授予新权限。
273  
274  删除成员前保留其结果和审计记录；未开始任务和被取消的未完成任务移交 Leader 重新决策。未投递消息保留失败/失效原因，不静默丢弃。工作目录含未汇总成果时不自动清理。
275  
276  ## 9. 持久化、恢复与取消
277  
278  ### 9.1 状态归属
279  
280  | 数据 | 权威保存位置 |
281  |---|---|
282  | TeamSpec 版本、团队事件、任务、运行意图、动作回执、批准、共享条目 | TeamAgents 的 SQLite 业务数据库 |
283  | 内置成员私有对话、规划、个体图执行与 interrupt 状态 | LangGraph 持久化 checkpointer |
284  | Codex 对话、执行线程和自身历史 | Codex 会话存储；TeamAgents 只保存对应线程与回合引用 |
285  | 文件成果、较大输出 | 工作目录或会话制品目录；事件保存引用、大小和必要摘要 |
286  | TUI 的筛选、展开和光标 | UI 状态，不能作为执行事实来源 |
287  
288  业务数据库使用单写入入口、事务及约束，启用 WAL 和合理 busy timeout；会话同时只允许一个运行实例取得执行所有权。LangGraph 使用文件型异步 SQLite checkpointer。检查点不手写、不复制私有对话到全局状态。[LangGraph Checkpointers](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/checkpointers)
289  
290  数据库必须对动作 ID、事件顺序、成员身份和投递批次建立唯一约束；任务状态更新检查预期旧状态。执行所有权使用本地会话文件锁，进程退出后释放；未知版本的数据库不能直接覆写，升级前备份并进行版本迁移。
291  
292  Team 控制图从业务数据库构建紧凑 TeamState，保存事件位置和状态引用，不再持久化第二份权威任务表。事件投影可重建；私有模型执行按对应检查点恢复。
293  
294  ### 9.2 提交和恢复顺序
295  
296  1. 接收用户输入或团队动作，先提交业务事务。
297  2. 持久化待执行 `TurnRun` 与输入投递批次，之后才启动成员。
298  3. 内置成员以稳定线程 ID 运行；关键写入采用同步持久化语义，先保存模型工具调用身份，再处理工具动作。
299  4. 团队动作单独即时提交；回合最终结果再提交 `TurnCompleted` 与任务状态。
300  5. 进程恢复后读取未终结 TurnRun，核对 LangGraph 检查点或 Codex 当前线程，不把 RUNNING 一律当成“从头重试”。
301  
302  必须覆盖四类崩溃窗口：提交后尚未启动、模型已完成但结果未归档、团队动作已提交但工具回执未保存、外部工具已执行但结果未知。
303  
304  团队内部动作可以凭 ID 去重；文件、Shell、远程 MCP/API 的外部副作用没有统一 exactly-once 保证。未知结果先检查文件、进程或远端状态；仍无法确认则保持 `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`，由 Leader 结合用户权限决定后续，不盲目重复执行。
305  
306  ### 9.3 暂停与取消
307  
308  `pause` 停止新的调度并在安全边界保存活动状态；`cancel` 请求停止指定任务/成员，工具进程按进程组管理，避免只结束 Shell 而遗留子进程。强制停止有明确超时和结果标记。
309  
310  取消不是回滚。已经修改的文件、发送的请求及部分制品继续保留；工作是否完成与是否需要清理独立处理。等待批准恢复时重新核对动作参数、配置版本和权限，不沿用已失效的批准。
311  
312  正常退出默认保存并暂停；异常退出通过下一次启动恢复。关闭终端后持续后台执行不作为首版保证；不为此新增守护进程系统。
313  
314  ## 10. Deep Agents 与 Codex 适配
315  
316  ### 10.1 内置成员
317

<message type="tool">501  ## 16. 开发顺序与每阶段交付
502  
503  | 阶段 | 交付内容 | 完成条件 |
504  |---|---|---|
505  | P0 接口验证 | 依赖锁定；独立 Deep Agents 线程；用户消息注入；interrupt/resume；Codex 握手、事件、批准、取消；Linux 隔离探针 | 验证框架能力与本方案一致，记录已知限制；不启动大规模业务实现 |
506  | P1 团队语义 | 配置、Task/TurnRun、动作事务、通信/观察 ACL、共享空间、纯代码调度；假成员 | T1～T6 的语义测试、单 Leader、违规操作和资源上限通过 |
507  | P2 持久运行 | 短控制图、异步成员执行、检查点、消息投递、暂停/取消、崩溃核对 | T2 真并行、T8 跨进程恢复、四类崩溃窗口通过 |
508  | P3 内置成员与五家模型 | Deep Agents middleware、模型 profiles、文件/Shell、MCP、网页、Skills、批准/全自动 | 五家真实工具调用闭环；越界批准与进程停止通过 |
509  | P4 Leader 与动态团队 | 自然语言组队、Leader 决策、版本 patch、受影响成员 drain、持续输入 | 单人成队/自动组队/变更/移除/改模型/执行中补充全部通过 |
510  | P5 Codex 与工作目录 | Codex 执行成员、持久线程、进度/批准/取消/恢复、共享/隔离/worktree | 混合团队闭环、Codex 异常恢复、合并冲突和成果保留通过 |
511  | P6 完整 TUI | 对话、成员/任务、详情、共享空间、批准、设置、会话管理 | 不阻塞输入；中英文、多行输入、缩放、键盘与恢复流程通过 |
512  | P7 发布验收 | 安装包、doctor、用户文档、样例、真实服务与故障回归 | 全部首版验收项通过，形成带版本的结果记录 |
513  
514  P1 起即可使用薄 TUI 验证交互，P6 完成全部视图；P0 必须先验证 Codex 与消息注入，避免到适配阶段才发现根本性限制。阶段允许按依赖并行开发，但不能把后续阶段降为“首版以后再做”。
515  
516  ## 17. 验收标准
517  
518  测试以行为和失败场景为单位；用脚本化假成员保证可重复性，真实 API 测试独立运行。并行测试用同步屏障证明两个成员同时开始，不能仅凭输出顺序或耗时猜测。
519  
520  以下场景中的 A、B、C 是独立成员的示例标识，Leader 单独标明；除明确测试 Codex 的场景外，默认使用内置成员。每个场景自行声明所需的 TeamSpec、权限和任务输入。
521  
522  | ID | 场景 | 必须满足的结果 |
523  |---|---|---|
524  | T1 | Leader 委派给 B 并汇总 | Task 有完整生命周期；结果只回委派者；Leader 交付 |
525  | T2 | B/C 并行 | 同时处于执行状态；一个慢任务不阻塞 Leader 处理新输入 |
526  | T3 | B/C 讨论 | 消息按允许通道送达，重复投递不重复注入；到达边界正确停止 |
527  | T4 | 观察者 | 只收到授权事件与载荷，不能因观察权限直接发消息或读私有历史 |
528  | T5 | 共享空间 | B 发布后 C 可发现；无读/写权限的成员被阻止；大输出使用引用 |
529  | T6 | 信息隔离 | A→B 私信不进入 C 视图；Leader 不自动获得私有对话；新会话不继承 |
530  | T7 | 多模型 | 五家各完成工具调用和续接；同队混用；更换配置不改变角色与 ACL |
531  | T8 | 恢复 | 杀死进程后恢复团队版本、待办、消息位置、成员线程和批准；不盲重放副作用 |
532  | T9 | 单 Leader | 不创建其他成员也可执行、交付并继续对话 |
533  | T10 | 自然语言组队 | Leader 输出合法结构；非法引用、未知模型或越权配置被拒绝并可修正 |
534  | T11 | 动态变更 | 普通成员只能提议；Leader patch 自动执行；受影响回合结束前不切换 |
535  | T12 | 版本冲突 | 两个基于旧版本的冲突 patch 不得相互覆盖或部分生效 |
536  | T13 | 移除与取消 | 停止后才移除；任务移交、结果与工作目录保留；无遗留工具进程 |
537  | T14 | 执行中补充 | B 持续运行时 Leader 接收补充；仅相关成员按边界调整 |
538  | T15 | 工具批准 | 预授权操作自动；越界暂停；其他成员继续；拒绝与过期批准正确处理 |
539  | T16 | 全自动 | 只能由用户开启；无需逐次批准；仍遵守系统权限、团队 ACL 和执行上限 |
540  | T17 | Codex 成员 | 任务、进度、最终结果、批准、取消与恢复映射正确；不直接获得团队操作权 |
541  | T18 | 工作目录 | shared/isolated/worktree 均通过；脏输入不被忽略；冲突不覆盖用户改动 |
542  | T19 | 工具生态 | 文件、Shell、网页搜索/抓取、MCP、Skills 分别完成真实任务；缺配置明确报错 |
543  | T20 | TUI | 流式输出期间输入、导航、批准仍可用；窄屏、多行中文和恢复正常 |
544  | T21 | 崩溃去重 | 动作提交后回执丢失仍只产生一次团队变更；投递重试不重复注入 |
545  | T22 | 资源和失败 | 限流、超时、单成员失败、死锁/无人就绪、超限均有可恢复状态 |
546  | T23 | 权限执行 | 路径穿越、符号链接、Shell 越界、MCP 未授权调用和数据库访问尝试不能绕过默认策略 |
547  | T24 | 会话复用 | 同一会话继续使用原成员上下文；同名新成员不继承旧身份；新会话隔离 |
548  
549  首版至少提供三类端到端示例：项目修改并测试、联网调研并附来源、文件/数据整理并交付制品。它们用于证明任务通用性，不作为产品支持范围的白名单。
550  
551  TUI 无模型部分使用 Textual 的测试设施；安全、恢复、适配器和事件状态转换需要针对性测试。无真实凭据时相关测试可被显式跳过，但“跳过”不能计作正式发布验收通过。
552  
553  ## 18. 开工前检查与完成定义
554  
555  开工前需要准备：Linux/Python 环境、可工作的 bubblewrap、Git、本机 Codex 登录、五类模型的可用配置，以及一个网页搜索/提取服务配置。密钥由使用者通过环境或本机配置提供，不进入仓库。
556  
557  P0 的关键验证结果必须包括：
558  
559  1. 所选 Deep Agents 版本的 middleware 能在不破坏工具调用配对的情况下接收新消息。
560  2. 独立线程的持久化暂停/恢复不会让其他成员停顿。
561  3. 所选 Codex 协议的批准、取消和断线核对可正确映射，生成的 Schema 与实际运行的 CLI 版本一致。
562  4. Linux 默认权限通过实际越界测试；环境不支持时能准确阻止不受限执行。
563  5. 五家模型的选定 profile 通过至少一次工具调用和续接；兼容层需要的额外字段得到保存。
564  
565  首版发布必须同时满足以下条件：Linux 上可安装启动；第 1 节规定的全部能力可从 TUI 使用；P0～P7 的交付完成；T1～T24 全部有验收证据；随产品提供配置、权限、恢复与故障处理文档。框架接口验证、依赖锁定和真实服务联调均属于实施交付，不能以方案评审代替。</message>
<message type="tool">  1  """Shared test helpers: team spec builders, scripted members, session harness."""
  2  
  3  from __future__ import annotations
  4  
  5  import asyncio
  6  from typing import Any
  7  
  8  import pytest
  9  
 10  from teamagents.agents import FakeMember
 11  from teamagents.models import (
 12      AgentSpec,
 13      ChannelMode,
 14      ChannelSpec,
 15      RuntimeKind,
 16      TeamSpec,
 17  )
 18  from teamagents.runtime import SessionRuntime, fake_session
 19  
 20  
 21  def member(agent_id: str, *, role: str = "worker", binds: list[str] | None = None,
 22             runtime: RuntimeKind = RuntimeKind.DEEPAGENTS, profile: str = "test") -&gt; AgentSpec:
 23      return AgentSpec(
 24          id=agent_id, name=agent_id.title(), role=role,
 25          runtime_kind=runtime, instructions=f"act as {agent_id}",
 26          model_profile=profile, tool_bindings=binds or ["files"],
 27      )
 28  
 29  
 30  def leader() -&gt; AgentSpec:
 31      return member("leader", role="leader", binds=["files", "shell", "web"])
 32  
 33  
 34  def spec_of(*agents: AgentSpec, channels: list[ChannelSpec] | None = None,
 35              observers=None, shared_spaces=None, limits=None) -&gt; TeamSpec:
 36      data: dict[str, Any] = {
 37          "leader_id": "leader",
 38          "agents": [a.model_dump() for a in agents],
 39          "channels": [c.model_dump() for c in (channels or [])],
 40      }
 41      if observers:
 42          data["observers"] = observers
 43      if shared_spaces:
 44          data["shared_spaces"] = shared_spaces
 45      if limits:
 46          data["limits"] = limits
 47      return TeamSpec.model_validate(data)
 48  
 49  
 50  def task_channel(source: str, targets: list[str]) -&gt; ChannelSpec:
 51      return ChannelSpec(source=source, targets=targets, mode=ChannelMode.TASK)
 52  
 53  
 54  def msg_channel(source: str, targets: list[str]) -&gt; ChannelSpec:
 55      return ChannelSpec(source=source, targets=targets, mode=ChannelMode.MESSAGE)
 56  
 57  
 58  def scripts(**members: list[tuple]) -&gt; dict[str, FakeMember]:
 59      return {name: FakeMember(name, script) for name, script in members.items()}
 60  
 61  
 62  class Harness:
 63      def __init__(self, rt: SessionRuntime, members: dict[str, FakeMember]):
 64          self.rt = rt
 65          self.members = members
 66  
 67      async def start(self):
 68          await self.rt.start()
 69          return self
 70  
 71      async def user(self, text: str):
 72          receipt = self.rt.user_message(text)
 73          assert receipt.ok, receipt.error
 74          return receipt
 75  
 76      async def settle(self, timeout: float = 8.0) -&gt; None:
 77          ok = await self.rt.settle(timeout)
 78          assert ok, "session did not settle: " + self.describe()
 79  
 80      def describe(self) -&gt; str:
 81          runs = self.rt.store.runs_for_session(
 82              self.rt.session_id, ["QUEUED", "RUNNING", "WAITING_TASK", "WAITING_APPROVAL"])
 83          return "; ".join(f"{r.agent_id}:{r.status}" for r in runs)
 84  
 85      async def aclose(self):
 86          await self.rt.close()
 87          self.rt.store.close()
 88  
 89  
 90  @pytest.fixture
 91  async def harness_factory(tmp_path):
 92      created: list[Harness] = []
 93  
 94      async def make(spec: TeamSpec, member_scripts: dict[str, FakeMember],
 95                     catalog=None, pre_authorized=None, require_approval=None,
 96                     tool_executor=None, session_id: str = "s1",
 97                     barriers: dict[str, asyncio.Event] | None = None) -&gt; Harness:
 98          for m in member_scripts.values():
 99              if barriers:
100                  m.barriers = {**m.barriers, **barriers}

<message type="tool">  1  """P2: persistence, restart recovery and the four crash windows.
  2  
  3  - queued execution intents survive a restart and still run
  4  - a RUNNING run without an external turn is re-queued; with one it becomes
  5    OUTCOME_UNKNOWN instead of being blindly retried
  6  - an action committed before a crash is not applied twice after the retry
  7  - acknowledged deliveries are never injected twice
  8  """
  9  
 10  from __future__ import annotations
 11  
 12  import asyncio
 13  
 14  from conftest import Harness, leader, member, msg_channel, scripts, spec_of, task_channel
 15  from teamagents.runtime import fake_session
 16  
 17  
 18  async def test_crash_after_action_commit_applies_once(harness_factory, tmp_path):
 19      """Crash window 3: the team action committed but the turn never finished.
 20      Re-running the turn replays the same action id -&gt; exactly one task."""
 21      spec = spec_of(leader(), member("b"), channels=[task_channel("leader", ["b"])])
 22  
 23      members = scripts(
 24          leader=[("call", "assign_task", {"assignee": "b", "description": "one task"}),
 25                  ("barrier", "hold"), ("call", "signal_done", {}), ("end",)],
 26      )
 27      h = await harness_factory(spec, members, barriers={"hold": asyncio.Barrier(2)})
 28      await h.user("go")
 29      # wait until assign_task committed, then simulate a hard process loss
 30      deadline = asyncio.get_event_loop().time() + 5
 31      while not h.rt.store.tasks_for_session("s1"):
 32          assert asyncio.get_event_loop().time() &lt; deadline
 33          await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
 34      await h.rt.close()  # process loss: in-flight task cancelled mid-turn
 35  
 36      # restart in a fresh runtime over the same database
 37      fresh = scripts(
 38          leader=[("call", "assign_task", {"assignee": "b", "description": "one task"}),
 39                  ("call", "signal_done", {}), ("end",)],
 40          b=[("call", "complete_task", {"task_id": "$inbox0.payload.task_id"}), ("end",)],
 41      )
 42      h2 = Harness(fake_session(tmp_path, spec, fresh, session_id="s1"), fresh)
 43      await h2.start()
 44      try:
 45          await h2.settle(8)
 46          tasks = h2.rt.store.tasks_for_session("s1")
 47          assert len(tasks) == 1, "replayed action must dedup, not create a second task"
 48          created = [e for e in h2.rt.store.events("s1") if e["kind"] == "task_created"]
 49          assert len(created) == 1
 50      finally:
 51          await h2.aclose()
 52  
 53  
 54  async def test_running_run_with_external_turn_becomes_outcome_unknown(tmp_path):
 55      spec = spec_of(leader(), channels=[])
 56      members = scripts(leader=[("sleep", 1), ("end",)])
 57      rt = fake_session(tmp_path, spec, members, session_id="s1")
 58      await rt.start()
 59      try:
 60          rt.user_message("go")
 61          deadline = asyncio.get_event_loop().time() + 5
 62          while not rt.store.runs_for_session("s1", ["RUNNING"]):
 63              assert asyncio.get_event_loop().time() &lt; deadline
 64              await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
 65          run = rt.store.runs_for_session("s1", ["RUNNING"])[0]
 66          # an external backend turn was submitted before the crash
 67          rt.store.set_run_status(run.run_id, run.status, external_turn_id="codex-turn-1")
 68      finally:
 69          await rt.close()
 70          rt.store.close()
 71  
 72      rt2 = fake_session(tmp_path, spec, scripts(leader=[("end",)]), session_id="s1")
 73      await rt2.start()
 74      try:
 75          await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
 76          run = rt2.store.get_run(run.run_id)
 77          assert run.status == "OUTCOME_UNKNOWN"
 78          assert not [r for r in rt2.store.runs_for_session("s1", ["RUNNING"])], \
 79              "unknown outcomes are never auto-retried"
 80      finally:
 81          await rt2.close()
 82          rt2.store.close()
 83  
 84  
 85  async def test_acked_deliveries_are_not_injected_twice(harness_factory, tmp_path):
 86      spec = spec_of(leader(), member("b"),
 87                     channels=[task_channel("leader", ["b"]), msg_channel("b", ["leader"])])
 88      members = scripts(
 89          leader=[("call", "assign_task", {"assignee": "b", "description": "job"}),
 90                  ("call", "wait_for_tasks", {"task_ids": ["$r0.result.task_id"]}),
 91                  ("wait",), ("call", "signal_done", {}), ("end",)],
 92          b=[("call", "complete_task", {"task_id": "$inbox0.payload.task_id"}), ("end",)],
 93      )
 94      h = await harness_factory(spec, members)
 95      await h.user("run the job")
 96      await h.settle()
 97      assert h.rt.store.pending_deliveries("s1", "b") == []
 98  
 99      # restart: nothing pending, nothing re-injected
100      fresh = scripts(leader=[], b=[])

<message type="tool">  1  """P1 guards: ACL/validation violations are rejected with readable errors,
  2  identity is runtime-injected, version conflicts never half-apply, and the
  3  turn budget stops runaway loops with LIMIT_REACHED."""
  4  
  5  from __future__ import annotations
  6  
  7  import asyncio
  8  import json
  9  
 10  from conftest import leader, member, msg_channel, scripts, spec_of, task_channel
 11  from teamagents.models import RuntimeKind, TeamAction
 12  
 13  
 14  def act(kind: str, actor: str, payload: dict, action_id: str = None, run_id: str = None):
 15      act.n += 1
 16      return TeamAction(action_id=action_id or f"t-{kind}-{actor}-{act.n}", session_id="s1",
 17                        actor_id=actor, run_id=run_id, kind=kind, payload=payload)
 18  act.n = 0
 19  
 20  
 21  async def test_violations_are_rejected(harness_factory):
 22      spec = spec_of(
 23          leader(), member("b"), member("c"),
 24          channels=[task_channel("leader", ["b"]), msg_channel("b", ["leader"])],
 25      )
 26      h = await harness_factory(spec, scripts(leader=[("end",)], b=[("end",)]))
 27  
 28      cases = [
 29          (act("assign_task", "b", {"assignee": "ghost", "description": "x"}),
 30           "unknown assignee"),
 31          (act("assign_task", "b", {"assignee": "leader", "description": "x"}),
 32           "not allowed to assign"),
 33          (act("send_message", "b", {"target": "leader", "text": "hi"}), None),  # allowed
 34          (act("send_message", "leader", {"target": "b", "text": "hi"}),
 35           "not allowed to message"),
 36          (act("signal_done", "b", {}), "only the Leader"),
 37          (act("apply_topology_patch", "b", {"operations": [{"op": "remove_agent",
 38                                                             "agent_id": "leader"}],
 39                                             "base_revision": 1}), "only the Leader"),
 40          (act("complete_task", "b", {"task_id": "nope"}), "unknown task"),
 41          (act("publish_shared", "b", {"space_id": "ghost", "content": "x"}), "unknown shared space"),
 42          (act("approval_decision", "b", {"approval_id": "x", "decision": "once"}),
 43           "only the local user"),
 44          (act("set_permission_mode", "leader", {"mode": "full_auto"}),
 45           "only the local user"),
 46      ]
 47      for i, (action, expect_error) in enumerate(cases):
 48          unique = action.model_copy(update={"action_id": f"{action.action_id}-{i}"})
 49          receipt = h.rt.submit(unique)
 50          if expect_error is None:
 51              assert receipt.ok, f"{action.kind} should be allowed"
 52          else:
 53              assert not receipt.ok and expect_error in (receipt.error or ""), \
 54                  f"{action.kind}: expected {expect_error!r}, got {receipt.error!r}"
 55  
 56      # identity is injected: a member cannot act as someone else via payload fields
 57      forged = h.rt.submit(act("send_message", "b",
 58                               {"target": "leader", "text": "x", "actor_id": "leader"}))
 59      assert forged.ok  # payload field ignored, actor is b
 60      events = [e for e in h.rt.store.events("s1") if e["kind"] == "message"]
 61      assert all(e["actor_id"] == "b" for e in events)
 62

218  
219      def note_stream_chunk(self, run_id: str, agent_id: str, text: str) -&gt; None:
220          """Transient model output for the UI (not persisted)."""
221          if self.stream_sink is not None and text:
222              self.stream_sink(run_id, agent_id, text)
223  
224      def set_stream_sink(self, sink: Callable[[str, str, str], None] | None) -&gt; None:
225          self.stream_sink = sink
226  
227      async def submit_async(self, action: TeamAction) -&gt; Receipt:
228          async with self.lock:
229              return self.submit(action)
230  
231      def user_message(self, text: str, action_id: str | None = None,
232                       supplement: bool = False) -&gt; Receipt:
233          return self.submit(TeamAction(
234              action_id=action_id or new_id("user"),
235              session_id=self.session_id,
236              actor_id="user",
237              kind=ActionKind.USER_SUPPLEMENT if supplement else ActionKind.USER_MESSAGE,
238              payload={"text": text},
239          ))
240  
241      def _drain_mid_turn(self) -&gt; None:
242          pushes = getattr(self.control, "mid_turn_pushes", [])
243          while pushes:
244              run_id, items = pushes.pop(0)
245              runner = None
246              run = self.store.get_run(run_id)
247              if run is not None:
248                  runner = self.runners.get(run.agent_id)
249              if runner is not None:
250                  runner.deliver_mid_turn(run_id, items)
251  
252      # ---------------------------------------------------------------- executor
253  
254      async def _loop(self) -&gt; None:
255          while not self._closed:
256              self._start_ready_runs()
257              if self._inflight:
258                  done, _ = await asyncio.wait(
259                      list(self._inflight.values()) + [asyncio.create_task(self._wake.wait())],
260                      return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED)
261                  self._wake.clear()
262              else:
263                  await self._wake.wait()
264                  self._wake.clear()
265  
266      def _start_ready_runs(self) -&gt; None:
267          session = self.store.get_session(self.session_id)
268          if session is None or SessionStatus(session["status"]) is SessionStatus.PAUSED:
269              return
270          spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)
271          leaders_busy = sum(1 for r in self._inflight.values() if not r.done())
272          workers_busy = 0
273          for run_id in self._inflight:
274              run = self.store.get_run(run_id)
275              # a turn parked on an approval or a wait does not hold an execution slot
276              if (run is not None and run.agent_id != spec.leader_id
277                      and run.status in (TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING)):
278                  workers_busy += 1
279          for run in self.store.runs_for_session(self.session_id,
280                                                 [TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING]):
281              if run.run_id in self._inflight:
282                  continue
283              if any(a.id == run.agent_id for a in spec.agents):
284                  if self._refresh_runner_if_stale(run.agent_id, spec):
285                      continue  # a fresh backend is ready on the next pass
286              if run.agent_id in self.runners:
287                  if run.agent_id == spec.leader_id:
288                      pass  # the Leader keeps its own slot
289                  elif workers_busy &gt;= spec.limits.max_parallel_workers:
290                      continue
291              elif self.runner_factory is not None:
292                  # members added by a topology patch get their backend on demand
293                  try:
294                      self.runners[run.agent_id] = self.runner_factory(
295                          spec.agent(run.agent_id))
296                  except Exception as e:
297                      self._finalize(run, TurnOutcome(
298                          status=TurnStatus.FAILED,
299                          error=f"cannot start member {run.agent_id!r}: {e}"))
300                      continue
301              else:
302                  continue
303              status = self.store.agent_status(self.session_id, run.agent_id)
304              if status is AgentStatus.REMOVED:
305                  continue
306              task = asyncio.create_task(self._execute(run), name=f"run-{run.run_id}")
307              self._inflight[run.run_id] = task
308              if run.agent_id != spec.leader_id:
309                  workers_busy += 1
310              leaders_busy += 1
311              _ = leaders_busy
312          self._watch_cancellations()
313  
314      def _watch_cancellations(self) -&gt; None:
315          """Ask the owning backend to stop runs the user/Leader cancelled."""
316          for run_id, task in list(self._inflight.items()):
317              if task.done() or run_id in self._cancel_tasks:
318                  continue
319              if not self.store.run_cancel_requested(run_id):
320                  continue
321              run = self.store.get_run(run_id)
322              runner = self.runners.get(run.agent_id) if run else None
323              if runner is None:
324                  continue
325              self._cancel_tasks[run_id] = asyncio.create_task(
326                  self._request_stop(run, runner), name=f"cancel-{run_id}")
327  
328      async def _request_stop(self, run: TurnRun, runner: AgentRunner) -&gt; None:
329          """Cancellation waits for the backend to confirm the stop, or marks
330          OUTCOME_UNKNOWN once the confirmation timeout expires."""
331          spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)
332          try:
333              await asyncio.wait_for(runner.request_interrupt(run.run_id),
334                                     timeout=spec.limits.cancel_confirm_timeout_s)
335          except asyncio.TimeoutError:
336              self.store.update_run_status_where(
337                  run.run_id, TurnStatus.RUNNING, TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN)
338          finally:
339              self._cancel_tasks.pop(run.run_id, None)
340              self._wake.set()
341

407      def _guarded_executor(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], Any]:
408          def execute(tool_name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -&gt; Any:
409              spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)
410              used = self._step_counts.get(run.run_id, 0) + 1
411              self._step_counts[run.run_id] = used
412              if used &gt; spec.limits.max_model_steps_per_turn:
413                  raise RuntimeError(
414                      f"step limit {spec.limits.max_model_steps_per_turn} reached for this turn")
415              if self.tool_executor is None:
416                  raise RuntimeError(f"no tool executor configured for {tool_name!r}")
417              return self.tool_executor(tool_name, args)
418          return execute

420      def _wake_info(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; WakeInfo:
421          decisions = self.store.decided_approvals_for_run(run.run_id)
422          if decisions:
423              return WakeInfo(reason="approval",
424                              payload={"decisions": [{"approval_id": d.approval_id,
425                                                      "status": d.status} for d in decisions],
426                                       "denied": any(d.status is ApprovalStatus.DENIED
427                                                     for d in decisions)})
428          kinds = self.store.delivery_event_kinds(run.input_delivery_ids)
429          if kinds and kinds[-1] == EventKind.USER_MESSAGE:
430              return WakeInfo(reason="user_input", payload={"kinds": kinds})
431          if run.waiting_on:
432              results = {tid: self._task_snapshot(tid) for tid in run.waiting_on}
433              return WakeInfo(reason="task_results",
434                              payload={"task_ids": run.waiting_on, "results": results})
435          return WakeInfo(reason="new_input", payload={})
436  
437      def _task_snapshot(self, task_id: str) -&gt; dict[str, Any]:
438          task = self.store.get_task(task_id)
439          if task is None:
440              return {"task_id": task_id, "status": "UNKNOWN"}
441          return {"task_id": task.task_id, "status": task.status,
442                  "result_refs": task.result_refs}

446      def _finalize(self, run: TurnRun, outcome: TurnOutcome) -&gt; None:
447          """Apply the turn result: completion requests, task semantics, deliveries."""
448          events: list[EventDraft] = []
449          req = self.store.completion_request(run.run_id)

117      async def start(self) -&gt; None:
118          self._closed = False
119          await self.reconcile()
120          self._loop_task = asyncio.create_task(self._loop(), name=f"ta-loop-{self.session_id}")
121  
122      async def close(self) -&gt; None:
123          self._closed = True
124          self._wake.set()
125          for task in list(self._inflight.values()):
126              task.cancel()
127          if self._inflight:
128              await asyncio.gather(*self._inflight.values(), return_exceptions=True)
129          if self._loop_task:
130              with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
131                  await self._loop_task
132          if self.cleanup is not None:
133              result = self.cleanup()
134              if asyncio.iscoroutine(result):
135                  await result
136  
137      async def reconcile(self) -&gt; None:
138          """After a restart: re-check runs that were in flight, never blind-retry side effects."""
139          for run in self.store.runs_for_session(self.session_id, [TurnStatus.RUNNING]):
140              runner = self.runners.get(run.agent_id)
141              state = runner.query_state(run.run_id) if runner is not None else None
142              if state is None and runner is not None and hasattr(runner, "reconcile"):
143                  state = await runner.reconcile(run)
144              if state is not None:
145                  self.store.set_run_status(run.run_id, state)
146                  continue
147              if run.external_turn_id:
148                  self.store.set_run_status(run.run_id, TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN)
149                  self._emit([EventDraft(kind=EventKind.RUN_FAILED,
150                                         payload={"run_id": run.run_id, "agent_id": run.agent_id,
151                                                  "status": "OUTCOME_UNKNOWN"})],
152                             actor_id="system")
153              else:
154                  # in-process runner only: safe to re-run the segment
155                  self.store.update_run_status_where(
156                      run.run_id, TurnStatus.RUNNING, TurnStatus.QUEUED)
157          for run in self.store.runs_for_session(self.session_id, [TurnStatus.QUEUED]):
158              _ = run
159          self._wake.set()

163      def submit(self, action: TeamAction) -&gt; Receipt:
164          """Synchronous submit through the single serialized control entry."""
165          receipt = self.control.submit(action)
166          if action.kind is ActionKind.APPROVAL_DECISION and receipt.ok:
167              self._deliver_approval_decision(action.payload["approval_id"],
168                                              action.payload["decision"])
169          self._drain_mid_turn()

173      def _deliver_approval_decision(self, approval_id: str, decision: str) -&gt; None:
174          """External backends (Codex) hold a live turn parked on the decision."""
175          approval = self.store.get_approval(approval_id)
176          if approval is None:
177              return
178          run = self.store.get_run(approval.run_id)
179          runner = self.runners.get(run.agent_id) if run else None
180          resolve = getattr(runner, "resolve_approval", None)
181          if resolve is not None:
182              resolve(approval_id, decision)

202          elif status is TurnStatus.RUNNING:

155  
156  def build_agent_view(store: Store, spec: TeamSpec, session_id: str, agent_id: str,
157                       run: TurnRun | None = None) -&gt; AgentView:
158      """What this member may see right now: assignments, inbox delta, shared delta."""
159      assignment = [t for t in store.tasks_for_session(session_id) if t.assignee == agent_id]
160      pending = store.pending_deliveries(session_id, agent_id)
161      inbox: list[dict] = []
162      delivery_ids: list[int] = []
163      batch_max = 0
164      for d in pending:
165          payload = (json.loads(d["payload_override"]) if d["payload_override"]
166                     else json.loads(d["payload_json"]))
167          inbox.append({
168              "event_id": d["event_id"],
169              "kind": d["event_kind"],
170              "from": d["event_actor"],
171              "task_id": d["event_task_id"],
172              "payload": payload,
173          })
174          delivery_ids.append(d["delivery_id"])
175          batch_max = max(batch_max, d["batch_no"])
176  
177      readable_spaces = [s.id for s in spec.shared_spaces
178                         if agent_id in s.readers or agent_id in s.writers]
179      shared_delta: list[SharedEntry] = []
180      for space_id in readable_spaces:
181          cursor = store.shared_cursor(session_id, agent_id, space_id)
182          shared_delta.extend(store.shared_entries(session_id, [space_id],
183                                                   after_sequence=cursor, limit=50))
184      capabilities = sorted(set(spec.agent(agent_id).tool_bindings))
185      return AgentView(
186          agent_id=agent_id,
187          assignment=assignment,
188          inbox_delta=inbox,
189          permitted_shared_delta=shared_delta,
190          relevant_topology={
191              "revision": store.current_revision(session_id),
192              "members": [{"id": a.id, "name": a.name, "role": a.role,
193                           "runtime_kind": a.runtime_kind, "status": store.agent_status(session_id, a.id)}
194                          for a in spec.agents],
195              "can_send_to": sorted({a.id for a in spec.agents
196                                     if spec.can_send(agent_id, a.id)}),
197              "can_delegate_to": sorted({a.id for a in spec.agents
198                                         if spec.can_delegate(agent_id, a.id)}),
199              "shared_spaces": readable_spaces,
200              "leader": spec.leader_id,
201          },
202          capabilities=capabilities,
203          delivery_ids=delivery_ids,
204          batch_no=batch_max,
205      )</message>

237  class TeamAgentMiddleware(AgentMiddleware):
238      """Injects authorized input, bounds the turn, enforces approvals."""
239  
240      name = "teamagents"
241      state_schema = TeamMemberState
242  
243      def __init__(self, *, runner: "DeepAgentsRunner", approvals: ApprovalGate,
244                   max_model_steps: int):
245          self.runner = runner
246          self.approvals = approvals
247          self.max_model_steps = max_model_steps
248          self.model_steps = 0
249  
250      def reset(self) -&gt; None:
251          self.model_steps = 0
252  
253      async def abefore_model(self, state, runtime):
254          if self.model_steps &gt;= self.max_model_steps:
255              return None
256          items, batch = self.runner.current_inbox().drain()
257          if not items:
258              return None
259          text = "\n".join(
260              "&lt;update from=\"{}\" kind=\"{}\"&gt;{}&lt;/update&gt;".format(
261                  i.get("from"), i.get("kind"),
262                  json.dumps(i.get("payload", {}), ensure_ascii=False))
263              for i in items)
264          return {"messages": [HumanMessage(content=text)], "applied_batch": batch}
265  
266      async def awrap_model_call(self, request, handler):
267          self.model_steps += 1
268          if self.model_steps &gt; self.max_model_steps:
269              raise TurnLimitExceeded(
270                  f"model-step limit {self.max_model_steps} reached for this turn")
271          return await handler(request)
272  
273      async def awrap_tool_call(self, request, handler):
274          tool_call = request.tool_call
275          name = tool_call["name"]
276          if name in TEAM_TOOLS:
277              result = await handler(request)
278              if name == "wait_for_tasks":
279                  # durable pause: the graph stops here and resumes on the same
280                  # run_id when wait conditions resolve (plan section 6.2)
281                  try:
282                      payload = json.loads(result.content)
283                  except Exception:

382      def _thread_id(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; str:
383          epoch = (run.context_ref or "ctx:0").rsplit(":", 1)[-1]

386      def _build_backend(self) -&gt; CompositeBackend:
387          routes: dict[str, Any] = {
388              # route keys end with '/' — CompositeBackend joins them verbatim
389              "/artifacts/": FilesystemBackend(root_dir=self.artifacts_dir,
390                                               virtual_mode=True),
391          }
392          for i, skills_dir in enumerate(self.skills_dirs):
393              if skills_dir.is_dir():
394                  routes[f"/skills/{i}/"] = FilesystemBackend(root_dir=skills_dir,
395                                                              virtual_mode=True)
396          for i, memory_file in enumerate(self.memory_files):
397              if memory_file.is_file():
398                  routes[f"/memory/{i}/"] = FilesystemBackend(
399                      root_dir=memory_file.parent, virtual_mode=True)
400          default = IsolatedShellBackend(self.workdir, artifacts_dir=self.artifacts_dir,
401                                         extra_rw=self.extra_rw, network=False)
402          return CompositeBackend(default=default, routes=routes)

404      def _shell_tool(self) -&gt; BaseTool:
405          runner = self
406  
407          @tool
408          async def shell(command: str, timeout: int = 120, network: bool = False,
409                    tool_call_id: Annotated[str, InjectedToolCallId] = "") -&gt; str:
410              """Run a shell command in the isolated Linux sandbox (no network by
411              default; network=true requires user approval). Output is capped and
412              long output is stored under /artifacts."""
413              # permission (including network escalation) is decided once, by
414              # TeamAgentMiddleware.awrap_tool_call, before this body runs
415              import asyncio
416              result = await asyncio.to_thread(
417                  run_isolated, command, workdir=runner.workdir, timeout=timeout,
418                  network=network, extra_rw=runner.extra_rw,
419                  artifact_dir=runner.artifacts_dir)
420              return json.dumps({"ok": True, "exit_code": result.exit_code,
421                                 "output": result.output,
422                                 "truncated": result.truncated,
423                                 "artifact": result.artifact}, ensure_ascii=False)
424  
425          return shell

440          tools = [*build_team_tools(self), self._shell_tool(),

461      async def start_or_resume(self, run: TurnRun, view: AgentView, gateway: ToolGateway,
462                                wake: WakeInfo | None) -&gt; TurnOutcome:
463          self._active_run = run
464          self._active_gateway = gateway
465          if self._bound_tools is None:
466              from .tools import build_bound_tools
467              try:
468                  self._bound_tools = await build_bound_tools(
469                      self.catalog, list(self.agent.tool_bindings))
470              except Exception:
471                  raise
472          graph = self._ensure_graph(run)
473          if self._middleware is not None:
474              self._middleware.reset()
475          inbox = self._inboxes.setdefault(run.run_id, Inbox())
476          # the rendered input is authoritative for what this segment already saw;
477          # anything pushed while the run is live is injected at the next model call
478          inbox.items.clear()
479          inbox.batch = view.batch_no
480          thread_id = self._threads.setdefault(run.run_id, self._thread_id(run))
481          cfg = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}
482          self._states[run.run_id] = TurnStatus.RUNNING
483          try:
484              paused = self._paused_kind.get(run.run_id)
485              if paused == "approval":
486                  allow = not (wake.payload.get("denied", False) if wake else False)
487                  inbox.push(view.inbox_delta, view.batch_no)
488                  graph_input = Command(resume={"allow": allow,
489                                                "decisions": (wake.payload.get("decisions", [])
490                                                              if wake else [])})
491              elif paused == "waiting":
492                  inbox.push(view.inbox_delta, view.batch_no)
493                  graph_input = Command(resume={"resumed": True,
494                                                "results": (wake.payload.get("results", {})
495                                                            if wake else {})})
496              else:
497                  text = render_view(view, wake, str(self.workdir))
498                  graph_input = {"messages": [HumanMessage(content=text)]}
499              result, reply = await self._stream_graph(graph, graph_input, cfg, run)
500          except TurnLimitExceeded as e:
501              self._states[run.run_id] = TurnStatus.FAILED
502              return TurnOutcome(status=TurnStatus.FAILED, error=str(e), note="turn_limit")
503          except Exception as e:
504              # a provider that rejects the configured reasoning effort maps to `max`
505              # once; the graph is then rebuilt with the accepted value
506              if (self._effort_fallback_used or not _looks_like_effort_error(e)
507                      or not self._switch_effort_to_max()):
508                  self._states[run.run_id] = TurnStatus.FAILED
509                  return TurnOutcome(status=TurnStatus.FAILED,
510                                     error=f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

 45  async def test_member_proposal_is_leader_decision(harness_factory):
 46      """T11: a member can only propose; the Leader applies; it takes effect."""
 47      spec = spec_of(leader(), member("b"),
 48                     channels=[task_channel("leader", ["b"]), msg_channel("b", ["leader"])])
 49      members = scripts(
 50          leader=[("inbox",), ("end",)],
 51          b=[("call", "propose_team_change", {
 52              "operations": [add_worker_op()], "rationale": "need a helper"}),
 53             ("call", "complete_task", {"task_id": "$inbox0.payload.task_id"}),
 54             ("end",)],
 55      )
 56      h = await harness_factory(spec, members, catalog=CATALOG)
 57      await h.user("please start")
 58      receipt = h.rt.submit(act("assign_task", "leader",
 59                                {"assignee": "b", "description": "propose a helper"}))
 60      assert receipt.ok
 61      await h.settle()
 62      patches = [e for e in h.rt.store.events("s1")
 63                 if e["kind"] == "topology_proposed"]
 64      assert patches, "member proposal must be recorded"
 65      proposal = h.rt.store.patches_in_status("s1", "PROPOSED")[0]
 66      applied = h.rt.submit(act("apply_topology_patch", "leader",
 67                                {"patch_id": proposal.patch_id}))
 68      assert applied.ok and applied.result["status"] == "APPLIED", applied.result
 69      assert json.loads(patches[0]["payload_json"])["proposer"] == "b", \
 70          "the proposal keeps its author for audit"
 71      spec_after = h.rt.store.load_team_spec("s1")
 72      assert any(a.id == "worker" for a in spec_after.agents), \
 73          "Leader application must create the proposed member"
 74      assert h.rt.store.current_revision("s1") &gt;= 2
 75  
 76  
 77  async def test_structural_change_waits_for_boundary(harness_factory):
 78      """T11: affected members drain first; the patch applies after their turn ends."""
 79      spec = spec_of(leader(), member("b"),
 80                     channels=[task_channel("leader", ["b"]), msg_channel("b", ["leader"])])
 81      members = scripts(
 82          leader=[("call", "assign_task", {"assignee": "b", "description": "slow"}),
 83                  ("call", "wait_for_tasks", {"task_ids": ["$r0.result.task_id"]}),
 84                  ("wait",), ("call", "signal_done", {}), ("end",)],
 85          b=[("sleep", 1.0),
 86             ("call", "complete_task", {"task_id": "$inbox0.payload.task_id"}),
 87             ("end",)],
 88      )
 89      h = await harness_factory(spec, members, catalog=CATALOG)
 90      await h.user("start the slow job")
 91      deadline = time.time() + 5
 92      while not [r for r in h.rt.store.runs_for_session("s1", ["RUNNING"])
 93                 if r.agent_id == "b"]:
 94          assert time.time() &lt; deadline
 95          await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
 96  
 97      patch = h.rt.submit(act("apply_topology_patch", "leader",
 98                              {"operations": [
 99                                  {"op": "update_agent", "agent_id": "b",
100                                   "changes": {"instructions": "be terse"}}],
101                               "base_revision": 1}))
102      assert patch.ok and patch.result["status"] == "WAITING_BOUNDARY", patch.result
103      assert h.rt.store.agent_status("s1", "b").value == "DRAINING"
104      assert h.rt.store.current_revision("s1") == 1, "no partial application"
105      assert h.rt.store.agent_config_revision("s1", "b") == 1
106  
107      await h.settle(10)
108      assert h.rt.store.current_revision("s1") == 2, "patch applies after the boundary"
109      assert h.rt.store.agent_config_revision("s1", "b") == 2
110      assert h.rt.store.agent_status("s1", "b").value == "IDLE"
111      assert "topology_applied" in [e["kind"] for e in h.rt.store.events("s1")]

40  class SessionRuntime:
41      """Owns execution for one session. Single writer, single executor loop."""
42  
43      def __init__(self, store: Store, session_id: str,
44                   catalog: UserConfig | None = None,
45                   runners: dict[str, AgentRunner] | None = None,
46                   approvals: ApprovalGate | None = None,
47                   tool_executor: Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], Any] | None = None,
48                   lock: asyncio.Lock | None = None,
49                   cleanup: Callable[[], Any] | None = None,
50                   runner_factory: Callable[[Any], AgentRunner] | None = None):
51          self.store = store
52          self.session_id = session_id
53          self.catalog = catalog or UserConfig()
54          self.runners: dict[str, AgentRunner] = runners or {}
55          self.approvals = approvals or ApprovalGate(
56              store, session_id, PermissionPolicy())
57          self.tool_executor = tool_executor
58          self.lock = lock or asyncio.Lock()
59          self.cleanup = cleanup
60          self.runner_factory = runner_factory
61          #: UI sinks; high-frequency deltas are coalesced by the consumer, never
62          #: persisted (plan section 13: bounded queue, coalesced refresh)
63          self.stream_sink: Callable[[str, str, str], None] | None = None
64          self.control = Control(store, session_id, self.catalog)
65          self._inflight: dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}
66          self._cancel_tasks: dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}
67          self._wake = asyncio.Event()
68          self._closed = False
69          self._loop_task: asyncio.Task | None = None
70          self._step_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
71          self._runner_revisions: dict[str, int] = {}
72  
73      # ------------------------------------------------------------- lifecycle
74  
75      def add_runner(self, agent_id: str, runner: AgentRunner) -&gt; None:
76          self.runners[agent_id] = runner
77  
78      def _refresh_runner_if_stale(self, agent_id: str, spec) -&gt; bool:
79          """Rebuild a member's backend when its config revision moved (model change,
80          tool change...). Returns True when a rebuild was scheduled (skip this run
81          until the swap completes)."""

AND revision=?\",\n            (session_id, revision),\n        ).fetchone()\n        if row is None:\n            raise KeyError(f\"no team spec revision {revision} for session {session_id!r}\")\n        data = json.loads(row[\"spec_json\"])\n        # sessions written before D-10 still carry the removed limit keys; drop\n        # them so old sessions keep loading (TeamSpec *files* stay strict)\n        limits = data.get(\"limits\")\n        if isinstance(limits, dict):\n            data[\"limits\"] = {k: v for k, v in limits.items() if k in Limits.model_fields}\n        return TeamSpec.model_validate(data)\n\n    # -- action dedup --------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def get_action_receipt(self, action_id: str) -&gt; Receipt | None:\n        row = self.conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT receipt_json FROM actions WHERE action_id=?\", (action_id,)\n        ).fetchone()\n        return Receipt.model_validate_json(row[\"receipt_json\"]) if row else None\n\n    def record_action(self, action_id: str, session_id: str, actor_id: str,\n                      run_id: str | None, kind: str, payload_hash: str, receipt: Receipt) -&gt; None:\n        self.conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO actions(action_id, session_id, actor_id, run_id, kind, payload_hash,\"\n            \" receipt_json, created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)\",\n            (action_id, session_id, actor_id, run_id, kind, payload_hash,\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

110  async def test_turn_budget_stops_runaway_with_limit_reached(harness_factory):
111      spec = spec_of(
112          leader(), member("b"), member("c"),
113          channels=[task_channel("leader", ["b"]), msg_channel("b", ["c"]),
114                    msg_channel("c", ["b"])],
115          limits={"max_parallel_workers": 2, "max_members": 8, "max_turns_per_goal": 3,
116                  "max_model_steps_per_turn": 10, "turn_active_timeout_s": 30},
117      )
118      # b &lt;-&gt; c ping-pong forever; the turn budget must stop the cascade
119      bounce = [("call", "send_message", {"target": "c", "text": "tick"}), ("end",)] * 5
120      reply = [("call", "send_message", {"target": "b", "text": "tick"}), ("end",)] * 5
121      members = scripts(
122          leader=[("call", "assign_task", {"assignee": "b", "description": "spam"}),
123                  ("wait",), ("end",)],
124          b=bounce,
125          c=reply,
126      )
127      h = await harness_factory(spec, members)
128      await h.user("go")
129      await h.settle()
130      kinds = [e["kind"] for e in h.rt.store.events("s1")]
131      assert "limit_reached" in kinds, "runaway scheduling must report LIMIT_REACHED"
132      runs = h.rt.store.runs_for_session("s1")
133      assert len(runs) &lt;= 3, f"turn budget must cap started runs, got {len(runs)}"
134

136  async def test_failed_dependency_blocks_dependents_and_notifies_leader(harness_factory):
137      """T22: no silent stalls — a failed dependency turns dependents BLOCKED and
138      the Leader is told, so it can re-plan instead of idling."""
139      spec = spec_of(leader(), member("b"), channels=[task_channel("leader", ["b"])])
140      members = scripts(
141          leader=[("call", "assign_task", {"assignee": "b", "description": "will fail",
142                                          "task_id": "task_a"}),
143                  ("end",),
144                  ("inbox",), ("end",),
145                  ("inbox",), ("end",)],
146          b=[("fail", "member crashed")],
147      )
148      h = await harness_factory(spec, members)
149      await h.user("start")
150      await h.settle()
151      first = h.rt.submit(act("assign_task", "leader",
152                              {"assignee": "b", "description": "depends on a",
153                               "task_id": "task_b", "dependencies": ["task_a"]}))
154      assert first.ok
155      await asyncio.sleep(0.2)
156      await h.settle()
157      kinds = [(e["kind"], json.loads(e["payload_json"])) for e in h.rt.store.events("s1")]
158      blocked = [p for k, p in kinds if k == "task_blocked" and p.get("task_id") == "task_b"]
159      assert blocked, "dependency failure must block the dependent task"
160      assert "task_a" in blocked[0]["reason"]
161      assert h.rt.store.get_task("task_b").status == "BLOCKED"
162      # the Leader is notified (its next turn saw the blocked notice), so it can re-decide
163      seen = [item for item in members["leader"].observed_inbox
164              if item.get("kind") == "task_blocked"]
165      assert seen and seen[0]["payload"]["task_id"] == "task_b", \
166          "Leader must receive the blocked notice"</message>

236          assert old_model.calls and new_model.calls, "both models served turns"
237          # identity and ACLs unchanged: same member id, same channel rights
238          assert rt.store.load_team_spec("s1").agent("b").model_profile == "other"

245  async def test_two_conflicting_patches_never_partially_apply(harness_factory):
246      """T12: a patch based on an old revision is rejected wholesale."""
247      spec = spec_of(leader(), member("b"), channels=[task_channel("leader", ["b"])])
248      h = await harness_factory(spec, scripts(leader=[("end",)], b=[("end",)]), catalog=CATALOG)
249      first = h.rt.submit(act("apply_topology_patch", "leader",
250                              {"operations": [add_worker_op("worker1")],
251                               "base_revision": 1}))
252      assert first.ok
253      conflicting = h.rt.submit(act("apply_topology_patch", "leader",
254                                    {"operations": [add_worker_op("worker2"),
255                                                    {"op": "set_space_acl",
256                                                     "space_id": "main",
257                                                     "readers": ["worker2"]}],
258                                     "base_revision": 1}))
259      assert not conflicting.ok and "stale" in conflicting.error
260      spec_after = h.rt.store.load_team_spec("s1")
261      assert [a.id for a in spec_after.agents] == ["leader", "b", "worker1"], \
262          "no partial application from the rejected patch"
263      assert h.rt.store.current_revision("s1") == 2

266  async def test_mid_execution_supplement_reaches_running_leader(harness_factory):
267      """T14: while B keeps running, the Leader takes in a supplement at its next
268      model call and only the affected member adjusts."""
269      spec = spec_of(leader(), member("b"),
270                     channels=[task_channel("leader", ["b"]), msg_channel("b", ["leader"])])
271      members = scripts(
272          leader=[("call", "assign_task", {"assignee": "b", "description": "long job"}),
273                  ("call", "wait_for_tasks", {"task_ids": ["$r0.result.task_id"]}),
274                  ("wait",),
275                  ("inbox",),
276                  ("call", "wait_for_tasks", {"task_ids": ["$r0.result.task_id"]}),
277                  ("wait",),
278                  ("call", "signal_done", {"summary": "adjusted to the supplement"}),
279                  ("end",)],
280          b=[("sleep", 1.0),
281             ("call", "complete_task", {"task_id": "$inbox0.payload.task_id"}),
282             ("end",)],
283      )
284      h = await harness_factory(spec, members, catalog=CATALOG)
285      await h.user("start the long job")
286      deadline = time.time() + 5
287      while not [r for r in h.rt.store.runs_for_session("s1", ["RUNNING"])
288                 if r.agent_id == "b"]:
289          assert time.time() &lt; deadline
290          await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
291      h.rt.user_message("补充：结果要包含一句话总结", supplement=True)
292      await h.settle(15)
293      observed = [i for i in members["leader"].observed_inbox
294                  if i.get("kind") == "user_message"]
295      assert observed and observed[-1]["payload"].get("supplement") is True, \
296          "the Leader must receive the supplement at its next model call"
297      b_receipts = members["b"].results
298      assert all("总结" not in str(r.result) for r in b_receipts), \
299          "the unaffected member was not interrupted with the supplement"
300      assert h.rt.store.get_session("s1")["goal_state"] == "done"</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "=== AgentRunner protocol + TurnOutcome/WakeInfo:\n\"\"\"Session runtime: member execution handles, concurrency, turn lifecycle.\n\nThe runtime consumes persisted execution intents (QUEUED TurnRuns) created by\nthe control flow, starts each member's own execution, and writes results back\nthrough one serialized path. It never waits for all members before reacting;\none member's crash or pause changes only that member's execution state.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport contextlib\nimport logging\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Callable\n\nfrom .agents import AgentRunner, FakeMember, TEAM_TOOLS, ToolGateway, TurnOutcome, WakeInfo\nfrom .control import Control, EventDraft\nfrom .models import (\n    ActionKind,\n    AgentStatus,\n    ApprovalStatus,\n    EventKind,\n    Receipt,\n    SessionStatus,\n    TaskStatus,\n    TeamAction,\n    TurnRun,\n    TurnStatus,\n    UserConfig,\n=== fake_session:\n                 tool_executor: Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], Any] | None = None,\n                 session_id: str = \"s1\") -&gt; SessionRuntime:\n    \"\"\"Build a ready-to-run session with scripted members (tests, examples).\"\"\"\n    from .models import PermissionMode\n\n    path = Path(tmpdir) / f\"{session_id}.db\"\n    store = Store(path)\n    if store.get_session(session_id) is None:\n        store.create_session(session_id, str(tmpdir), \"approved_scope\")\n        store.save_team_spec(session_id, spec)\n        for agent in spec.agents:\n            store.ensure_agent(session_id, agent.id)\n    policy = PermissionPolicy(mode=PermissionMode.APPROVED_SCOPE,\n                              pre_authorized=pre_authorized,\n                              require_approval=require_approval)\n    runtime = SessionRuntime(store, session_id, catalog or UserConfig(),\n                             runners=dict(runners),\n                             approvals=ApprovalGate(store, session_id, policy),\n                             tool_executor=tool_executor)\n    return runtime\n\n\n_ = (TEAM_TOOLS, FakeMember)\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

  <content>Now let me write and run a deterministic probe script covering my four main hypotheses.</content>
  <tool_call id="call_00_ar1G3SFqZdRQAa2C0Tlo0986" name="write_file">{"content": "\"\"\"Core-domain review probes (read-only wrt the repo; writes only temp DBs).\n\nA) DRAINING leak: a patch that changes only *permissions* marks an idle-at-boundary\n   member DRAINING and never clears it -&gt; the member is skipped forever.\nB) Exception inside Control.submit's tx: a failure receipt is committed while a\n   write already performed inside _reduce stays committed (\"nothing half-applies\").\nD) Runtime.reconcile applies a terminal run status without _finalize: task stays\n   RUNNING, agent stays BUSY, no events, completion request never applied.\nC) TurnRun status is committed before deliveries are acked: a crash in between\n   leaves a terminal run with pending deliveries -&gt; next scheduling pass creates a\n   new run that re-injects the same input into the member's context.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nREPO = Path(\"/home/rimuru/Projects/Code/for_fun/TeamAgents\")\nsys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / \"src\"))\nsys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / \"tests\"))\n\nfrom teamagents.control import Control  # noqa: E402\nfrom teamagents.models import (  # noqa: E402\n    ActionKind, AgentSpec, AgentStatus, ChannelMode, ChannelSpec, RuntimeKind,\n    Task, TaskStatus, TeamAction, TeamSpec, TurnRun, TurnStatus, UserConfig,\n)\nfrom teamagents.storage import Store  # noqa: E402\n\nOUT: list[str] = []\n\n\ndef say(*parts) -&gt; None:\n    line = \" \".join(str(p) for p in parts)\n    OUT.append(line)\n    print(line, flush=True)\n\n\ndef agent(aid: str, role: str = \"worker\") -&gt; AgentSpec:\n    return AgentSpec(id=aid, name=aid.title(), role=role, runtime_kind=RuntimeKind.DEEPAGENTS,\n                     instructions=\"x\", model_profile=\"test\", tool_bindings=[\"files\"])\n\n\ndef build_spec() -&gt; TeamSpec:\n    return TeamSpec(\n        leader_id=\"leader\",\n        agents=[agent(\"leader\", \"leader\"), agent(\"b\"), agent(\"c\")],\n        channels=[\n            ChannelSpec(source=\"leader\", targets=[\"b\", \"c\"], mode=ChannelMode.TASK),\n            ChannelSpec(source=\"b\", targets=[\"c\"], mode=ChannelMode.MESSAGE),\n            ChannelSpec(source=\"c\", targets=[\"b\"], mode=ChannelMode.MESSAGE),\n        ],\n        shared_spaces=[{\"id\": \"main\", \"readers\": [\"leader\", \"b\"], \"writers\": [\"leader\", \"b\"]}],\n    )\n\n\ndef fresh_store(tag: str) -&gt; Store:\n    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f\"probe-{tag}-\"))\n    store = Store(tmp / \"team.db\")\n    store.create_session(\"s1\", str(tmp), \"approved_scope\")\n    spec = build_spec()\n    store.save_team_spec(\"s1\", spec)\n    for a in spec.agents:\n        store.ensure_agent(\"s1\", a.id)\n    return store\n\n\ndef act(kind, actor, payload, action_id):\n    return TeamAction(action_id=action_id, session_id=\"s1\", actor_id=actor, kind=kind,\n                      payload=payload)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- A\ndef probe_a() -&gt; None:\n    say(\"\\n== A) DRAINING leak (permission-only affected member) ==\")\n    store = fresh_store(\"a\")\n    control = Control(store, \"s1\")\n    run = TurnRun(run_id=\"run_c\", session_id=\"s1\", agent_id=\"c\", config_revision=1,\n                  topology_revision=1, status=TurnStatus.RUNNING)\n    store.insert_run(run)\n    store.set_agent_status(\"s1\", \"c\", AgentStatus.BUSY)\n    receipt = control.submit(act(ActionKind.APPLY_TOPOLOGY_PATCH, \"leader\",\n                                 {\"base_revision\": 1,\n                                  \"operations\": [{\"op\": \"add_channel\",\n                                                  \"channel\": {\"source\": \"c\",\n                                                              \"targets\": [\"b\", \"leader\"],\n                                                              \"mode\": \"message\"}}]},\n                                 \"A-patch\"))\n    say(\"A1 receipt:\", receipt.ok, receipt.result)\n    say(\"A2 affected_agents:\", receipt.result.get(\"affected_agents\"),\n        \"| c status:\", store.agent_status(\"s1\", \"c\"))\n    store.update_run_status_where(\"run_c\", TurnStatus.RUNNING, TurnStatus.COMPLETED)\n    control.schedule()\n    say(\"A3 after boundary: revision =\", store.current_revision(\"s1\"),\n        \"| c status =\", store.agent_status(\"s1\", \"c\"),\n        \"| c config_revision =\", store.agent_config_revision(\"s1\", \"c\"))\n    # b sends c a message: c has a pending delivery but is skipped by _schedule\n    msg = control.submit(act(ActionKind.SEND_MESSAGE, \"b\", {\"target\": \"c\", \"text\": \"hi\"}, \"A-msg\"))\n    control.schedule()\n    runs_c = [r for r in store.runs_for_session(\"s1\", [\"QUEUED\", \"RUNNING\"])\n              if r.agent_id == \"c\"]\n    say(\"A4 message delivered_to:\", msg.result, \"| pending deliveries for c:\",\n        len(store.pending_deliveries(\"s1\", \"c\")), \"| new runs for c:\", len(runs_c),\n        \"| c status:\", store.agent_status(\"s1\", \"c\"))\n    say(\"A5 VERDICT: DRAINING leak\" if store.agent_status(\"s1\", \"c\") is AgentStatus.DRAINING\n        and not runs_c and store.pending_deliveries(\"s1\", \"c\") else \"A5 VERDICT: no leak\")\n    store.close()\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- B\ndef probe_b() -&gt; None:\n    say(\"\\n== B) _reduce exception commits an already-performed write ==\")\n    store = fresh_store(\"b\")\n    control = Control(store, \"s1\")\n    original = Store.add_shared_entry\n\n    def boom(self, entry, session_id):\n        original(self, entry, session_id)      # the write
